### [1] [Coupling of TQFTs to QFTs and SymTFT interfaces from bordisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25012)
*Max Hübner,Ling Lin,Daniel Robbins,Subham Roy*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨 M-theory 局域模型中的非平凡拓扑（如挠循环）如何通过几何配边（bordism）在标准退耦极限下产生额外的拓扑扇区，并把这些扇区与局域量子场论耦合。从对称性拓扑场论（SymTFT）角度看，此类局域模型的物理边界条件被修改。作者提出一种由局部奇点与渐近边界之间的几何配边构造 SymTFT 界面的方法，并将其应用于冻结 D_5 奇点的圆紧化，识别出全理论 Z_4 SymTFT 与其局域 su(2) 规范扇区 Z_2 SymTFT 之间的拓扑界面，从而解释了冻结理论中电、磁对称表观不匹配的来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部 M-theory 模型中的挠循环和非平凡拓扑会连接奇点处局域化的量子场论扇区与局域模型的渐近区域。作者指出，这类结构在标准退耦极限下会引入额外的拓扑扇区，并与局域 QFT 产生非平凡耦合。从 SymTFT 视角看，这导致物理边界条件被修改，因此需要一种统一的几何构造来刻画此类界面及其对称性结构。

Method: 作者利用几何配边（geometric bordism）构造介于局部奇点与渐近边界之间的 SymTFT 界面。具体做法是：在退耦极限下，把由非平凡拓扑产生的额外拓扑扇区嵌入 SymTFT 框架，并用几何配边刻画全理论 SymTFT 与局域理论 SymTFT 之间的拓扑界面。该方法被应用于冻结 D_5 奇点的圆紧化（circle compactification）例子。

Result: 在冻结 D_5 奇点的圆紧化中，作者识别出一个位于全理论 Z_4 SymTFT 与局域 su(2) 规范理论 Z_2 SymTFT 之间的拓扑界面。进一步表明，规范理论与该额外拓扑量子场论之间的耦合能够解释冻结理论中电、磁对称性之间表观不匹配的现象。

Conclusion: M-theory 局域模型中的非平凡拓扑在退耦极限下会修改物理边界条件，并可通过几何配边构造 SymTFT 界面。该方法成功解释了冻结 D_5 奇点例子中电、磁对称性的表观不匹配，为理解局域模型与全理论之间的拓扑耦合提供了系统的几何框架。

Abstract: M-theory local models may exhibit torsional cycles and other non-trivial topology which interpolate between quantum field theory sectors localized at singularities and the asymptotic regions of the local model. We show that such structures give rise, in standard decoupling limits, to additional topological sectors which can couple non-trivially to the localized QFT. From a symmetry topological field theory (SymTFT) perspective, the physical boundary conditions associated to such local models are modified. We give a construction of SymTFT interfaces from geometric bordisms interpolating between the local singularity and the asymptotic boundary. We apply this construction to the circle compactification of a frozen $D_5$ singularity, identifying a topological interface between the $\mathbb{Z}_4$ SymTFT of the full theory and the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ SymTFT of its local $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ gauge sector. We show that the resulting coupling between the gauge theory and an additional topological quantum field theory accounts for the apparent mismatch between electric and magnetic symmetries of the frozen theory.

</details>


### [2] [Scherk-Schwarz Compactifications: From Circles to Strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25014)
*Karim Benakli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于Scherk-Schwarz紧致化的入门综述，以研究生可能自然提出的一系列问题为线索，从基础场论逐步延伸到超引力、轨形、量子效应和弦论，旨在为进入该文献领域提供所需的概念、计算与形式工具，不涉及唯象应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望回答一个简单问题：要进入Scherk-Schwarz紧致化的文献，需要掌握哪些预备知识。其动机是为初学者（尤其是研究生）提供一条清晰、循序渐进的学习路径，降低进入这一技术性较强领域的门槛。

Method: 本文采用综述形式，将内容组织为一系列研究生可能自然追问的问题，并围绕每个问题给出相应的概念、计算步骤和形式化工具。讨论从基础场论层面出发，逐步深入到超引力、轨形（orbifolds）、量子效应以及弦论等更进阶的课题，同时刻意略去唯象应用。

Result: 文章构建了一个由浅入深的认知框架，将Scherk-Schwarz紧致化所涉及的核心概念、计算方法与形式工具按难度梯度串联起来，使读者能够系统性地掌握进入相关文献所需的必要知识体系。

Conclusion: 该综述通过"提问—解答"的结构，为希望进入Scherk-Schwarz紧致化文献的研究生提供了从基础场论到弦论的完整知识路线图，并明确将范围限定在理论工具层面，不讨论唯象应用。

Abstract: This review is written with a simple question in mind: what does one need to know to enter the literature on Scherk-Schwarz compactifications? It is organized as a sequence of questions that a graduate student might naturally ask, together with the concepts, calculations, and formal tools needed to work through the literature. The discussion starts at an elementary field-theory level and gradually develops toward more advanced aspects of supergravity, orbifolds, quantum effects, and string theory. Phenomenological applications are deliberately left aside.

</details>


### [3] [Quiver-Invariant Triality between Brane Brick Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25017)
*Dongwook Ghim,Juno Kwon,Rak-Kyeong Seong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 2d (0,2) 超对称规范理论中由 brane brick model 描述的 quiver-invariant triality，找到一对具有相同规范群、场含量和 E-项但 J-项不同的对偶理论，并通过具体的 triality 变换序列将其联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: brane brick model 与 toric Calabi-Yau 4-流形关联的 2d (0,2) 理论之间的对偶关系尚未完全理解，特别是 triality 变换能否在保持 quiver（规范群与场含量）不变的情况下仅改变 J-项和 E-项，即 quiver-invariant triality 是否存在及其构造方式，是亟待澄清的问题。

Method: 作者构造了一对显式的 brane brick model，它们共享相同的规范群、场含量和 E-项，但 J-项不同；随后识别出连接这对模型的 triality 变换（mutation）序列。该序列在变换过程中使 quiver 回到其原始形式，同时改变 J-项和 E-项。作者还进一步阐述了这一 quiver-invariant triality 与 quiver-invariant brane tiling 的 orbifold 约化之间的关系。

Result: 论文给出了一对显式的 brane brick model 作为对偶例子，并确定了连接它们的 triality mutation 序列；该序列证明存在一类 quiver-invariant triality，能保持规范群、场含量及 E-项不变而只改变 J-项。同时建立了 quiver-invariant triality 与 quiver-invariant brane tiling 的 orbifold 约化的对应关系。

Conclusion: quiver-invariant triality 是 2d (0,2) 超对称规范理论中的一种重要对偶类型，它可以在保持 quiver 结构不变的情况下仅改变 J-项；这种对偶可通过与 orbifold 约化的联系加以理解，深化了对 brane brick model 对偶关系结构的认识。

Abstract: We study quiver-invariant triality between 2d (0,2) supersymmetric gauge theories described by brane brick models associated with toric Calabi-Yau 4-folds. The dual theories share the same gauge groups, field content and E-terms but have distinct J-terms. We present an explicit pair of such brane brick models and identify the sequence of triality mutations relating them. This sequence returns the quiver to its original form while transforming the J- and E-terms. We explain the relation between quiver-invariant triality and the orbifold reduction of quiver-invariant brane tilings.

</details>


### [4] [Asymmetric Orbifolds and 4-dimensional $\mathcal N=4$ String Islands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25020)
*Gerardo Aldazabal,Eduardo C. Andrés,Anamaría Font,Elisa I. Marieni,Kumar Narain,Ida G. Zadeh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了保持全部16个右移超对称的杂化弦与II型弦的 $\mathbb T^6/\mathbb Z_n$ 非对称轨形，得到除膨胀子外无任何无质量模的4维“岛屿”，并系统分类了这些岛屿及其“群岛”结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦论紧致化中，无质量标量模（moduli）的存在意味着理论存在连续退化族，难以得到唯一、可预测的4维真空。寻找除膨胀子外不含任何无质量模的4维真空（即“岛屿”），是理解弦景观结构与实现稳定紧致化的重要动机。

Method: 作者构造杂化弦和II型弦在 $\mathbb T^6/\mathbb Z_n$ 上的非对称轨形（asymmetric orbifold），并保持全部16个右移超对称。关键工作是验证轨形共形场论的一致性，包括算子解释（operator interpretation）的有效性。对杂化情形，基于Leech格构造的 $\Gamma(22,6)$ 格对所有岛屿分类；对II型情形，给出允许的 $\Gamma(6,6)$ 格及轨形生成元的分类。此外还检验特定轨形是否通过轨形作用的不同等价实现而给出“群岛”（archipelago）。

Result: 成功构造出除膨胀子外无任何无质量模的4维岛屿；建立了轨形共形场论的一致性（含算子解释有效性）；在杂化情形基于Leech格对全部岛屿完成分类，在II型情形给出允许的 $\Gamma(6,6)$ 格与轨形生成元的完整分类，并进一步研究了由轨形作用不同实现所产生的群岛结构。

Conclusion: 通过非对称轨形构造可得到保持16个右移超对称、且除膨胀子外无无质量模的4维岛屿；轨形共形场论具有严格一致性。该工作为理解无模弦真空的谱结构与分类提供了系统框架，杂化与II型两种情形中的格分类及群岛现象得到了明确刻画。

Abstract: We formulate heterotic and type II $\mathbb T^6/\mathbb Z_n$ asymmetric orbifolds preserving all 16 right-moving supersymmetries and leading to 4-dimensional islands without massless moduli other than the dilaton. We establish the consistency of the orbifold conformal field theory, including the validity of operator interpretation. In the heterotic case we classify all islands based on the $Γ(22,6)$ lattice constructed via the Leech lattice. In the type II case we also provide a classification of allowed $Γ(6,6)$ lattices and orbifold generators. In both cases we further examine whether a particular orbifold admits an archipelago of different islands arising from inequivalent realizations of the orbifold action.

</details>


### [5] [Conformal manifolds of $\mathcal{N}=1$ orbifold theories - Part I: Marginal deformations and the one-loop spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25021)
*Fabio Mantegazza,Elli Pomoni,Torben Skrzypek*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究保持最小超对称的四维 N=4 超杨-米尔斯理论轨形（orbifold），在平面极限下确定其共形流形，并构造完整的平面单圈伸缩算符，从而获得非 BPS 谱的微扰信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解 N=4 超杨-米尔斯理论轨形（保留最小超对称）的共形流形结构及其非 BPS 谱，为后续研究共形流形上的特殊点（Part II）奠定基础。

Method: 作者考虑平面极限下的两类 Abel 轨形族：计算其超共形指标（superconformal indices），构造完整的单迹 BPS 谱，并从规范理论和弦论两方面推导规范耦合的数目；识别的共形流形由规范耦合的一个子集和一个普适的 Leigh-Strassler 型超势形变张成，进而构造该流形上完整的平面单圈伸缩算符（dilatation operator）。

Result: 确定了两类 Abel 轨形的共形流形：由规范耦合的一个子集（其数目由规范理论和弦论两方面一致导出）与一个普适 Leigh-Strassler 型超势形变共同张成；构造了共形流形上完整的平面单圈伸缩算符，获得远离轨形点处全部非 BPS 谱的微扰可及性。

Conclusion: 本文为四维最小超对称轨形理论建立了共形流形与非 BPS 谱的微扰框架，为后续 Part II 中研究共形流形上的特殊点提供了系统工具和基础。

Abstract: We consider orbifolds of 4d $\mathcal{N}=4$ super-Yang--Mills theory that preserve minimal supersymmetry and study their conformal manifolds, focusing on two families of Abelian orbifolds in the planar limit. We compute their superconformal indices, construct their complete single-trace BPS spectra and identify the conformal manifolds. These are generically spanned by a subset of the gauge-couplings, whose number we derive from both gauge and string theory, and a universal Leigh--Strassler-type superpotential deformation. We then construct the complete, planar one-loop dilatation operator on these conformal manifolds, giving perturbative access to the full non-BPS spectrum away from the orbifold point. This lays the groundwork for studying special loci of the conformal manifold in a forthcoming Part II.

</details>


### [6] [Stringy Corrections from Gravity: Predictions for BFSS and ABJM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25022)
*Aidan Herderschee,Victor Ivo,Henry W. Lin,Yifan Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从 11 维超引力在圆周上的单圈绕弦模式出发，利用弦/M 理论对偶关系，闭合地推导出 IIA 型有效作用量中关于度规、伸缩子和 Ramond–Ramond 一形式的领头阶树级弦修正项；该方法在热 D0-膜几何上给出 BFSS 矩阵模型低温热力学的首个修正，其系数与格点蒙特卡洛估计相差约 20%，并在包含四形式通量时重现了局域化得到的 ABJM 三球自由能中的 ζ(3)k² 项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管弦理论取得巨大进展，许多全息可观测量领头阶弦修正仍然未知；作者希望利用弦/M 理论对偶，仅凭引力即可确定这些未知的弦修正，从而填补这一空白。

Method: 利用弦/M 理论对偶，从 11 维超引力在圆环上的单圈绕弦模式出发，推导 IIA 型有效作用量中关于度规、伸缩子和 Ramond–Ramond 一形式的领头阶树级弦项，结果表示为洛伦兹生成元上的有限超迹的闭合形式；并将该方法分别应用于热 D0-膜几何（含/不含四形式通量）以检验其对 BFSS 矩阵模型和 ABJM 理论的具体预言。

Result: 该方法给出 BFSS 矩阵模型低温热力学的首个修正，其系数与已发表的格点蒙特卡洛估计相差约 20%；在包含四形式通量时，同一方法成功重现了局域化得到的 ABJM 三球自由能中的 ζ(3)k² 项。

Conclusion: 通过弦/M 理论对偶，仅凭引力即可确定许多此前未知的全息可观测量的领头阶弦修正，且该方法在不同理论（BFSS 矩阵模型与 ABJM 理论）的检验中表现出良好的一致性，为系统确定弦修正提供了通用途径。

Abstract: Despite enormous progress in string theory, the leading stringy corrections to many holographic observables remain unknown. String/M-theory dualities nonetheless allow them to be determined from gravity alone. From the winding modes of one-loop 11d supergravity on a circle, we derive the leading tree-level stringy terms in the type IIA effective action for the metric, dilaton, and Ramond--Ramond one-form, in closed form as a finite supertrace over Lorentz generators. On the thermal D0-brane geometry, this gives the first correction to the low-temperature thermodynamics of the BFSS matrix model. Its coefficient differs from published lattice Monte Carlo estimates by $\approx 20\%$. With the four-form flux included, the same method reproduces the $ζ(3)k^{2}$ term of the ABJM free energy on the three-sphere obtained by localization.

</details>


### [7] [Actionless Branes on Axion Wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25026)
*A. Ramesh Chandra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明Calabi-Yau紧化中看似违反"虚距离界"的欧几里得轴子虫洞，实际上仍会因轴子间的束缚态（如带弦荷的五膜、D5-NS5束缚态）在到达无穷远之前变得无作用量，从而符合凸包轴子弱引力猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面上看，Calabi-Yau紧化中多个轴子共享场偏移，使任意单轴子荷的瞬子全程保持压低，似乎违反了虚距离界（该界要求某瞬子必先变得无作用量）。本文旨在解决这一矛盾，说明虫洞无法逃脱所有瞬子的约束。

Method: 作者指出虫洞可避开单个轴子的瞬子，但无法避开其束缚态：中心荷错位使得束缚态比其组成部分更便宜，而轴子作用于每一个组分。在IIB型弦论中，由Neveu-Schwarz二形式的两个轴子支撑的虫洞上，携带弦荷的五膜在大体积下、距无穷远三分之二处即变得无作用量；通过S对偶可分别对应带磁通的D5膜或D5-NS5束缚态。

Result: 无论轴子相对符号如何、如何分担场偏移，携带弦荷的五膜都会在任意Calabi-Yau三重流形上变得无作用量，且增加更多轴子无济于事；决定因素在于saxion扇区的齐次度，而非轴子数目。

Conclusion: 这些轴子束缚态正是凸包轴子弱引力猜想所要求的对象，从而恢复了虚距离界的一致性，证明虫洞无法在保持所有瞬子压低的同时延伸到无穷远。

Abstract: Can a Euclidean axion wormhole extend to infinity with every instanton still suppressed? The imaginary distance bound says it cannot: some instanton must become actionless first. Calabi-Yau compactifications appear to violate the bound: several axions share the field excursion, and the instantons charged under any one of them stay suppressed all the way. We show that the wormhole can hide from these instantons but not from their bound states: misaligned central charges make a bound state cheaper than its constituents, while the axions act on every constituent. On the type IIB wormhole supported by two axions of the Neveu-Schwarz two-form, a fivebrane carrying string charge becomes actionless at large volume within two thirds of the way to infinity, for either relative sign of the axions and however they share the excursion. In S-dual variables it is a D5-brane with magnetic flux for one sign and a D5-NS5 bound state for the other. Adding more axions does not help: the same branes become actionless on every Calabi-Yau threefold, because what counts is the degree of homogeneity of the saxion sector, not the number of axions. These bound states are what the convex-hull axion weak gravity conjecture requires.

</details>


### [8] [Twist and Shout: defect lines on the Rényi torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25032)
*Naveen Balaji Umasankar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文考虑临界系统中两区域间的纠缠如何揭示其对称性（包括不可逆对称性），通过将拓扑缺陷线缠绕于二维共形场论中的一个区间，定义了一个仅依赖交叉比的观测量，并证明它能探测't Hooft反常、区分具有相同融合规则的融合范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨纠缠（特别是第二Rényi熵在环面上的配分函数刻画）能否编码临界系统对称性的信息，尤其是超出可逆群对称的不可逆对称（如融合范畴），并研究观测量在区间靠近时的行为与相关算符的自旋约束的联系。

[1] by reproducing the exact CFT expression of the OTOC from the bulk and explaining why it exhibits an exponential growth {\it only} when the operators lie on the side of the two fixed points.

Abstract: How chiral phonons survive anharmonic phonon-phonon interactions, which underpin lattice dynamics at finite temperature, remains poorly understood. Using an ab initio many-body approach, we study crystalline tellurium (Te), a strongly anharmonic helical crystal. We show that rotational symmetries in Te impose pseudoangular momentum selection rules on phonon-phonon interactions, restricting mode mixing and scattering at rotationally invariant symmetry points in the Brillouin zone. These symmetries protect the circular polarization of vibrational modes at finite temperature but not their lifetimes such that the dynamic chirality of handed vibrational modes persists to 600 K, even as lifetimes drop sharply. We find that the temperature-dependence of angular momentum generated by a thermal gradient is governed by lifetimes rather than chirality, decreasing by two orders of magnitude between 10 and 600 K.

TL;DR: 本文利用平面波基组下的辅助场量子蒙特卡罗（AFQMC，PAW 框架）计算硅自间隙与空位的形成能，并与 RPA、CCSD(T) 对比，发现此前 CCSD(T) 对四面体自间隙的高估源于有限尺寸处理而非缺陷的多组态特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点缺陷能量学的精确预测仍是计算材料科学的难题：四面体硅自间隙的形成能，周期性 CCSD(T) 与扩散蒙特卡罗（DMC）结果相差约 1.2 eV。需要厘清这一分歧的物理来源（有限尺寸效应还是电子结构多组态特征），并给出更可靠的参考值。

Result: RPA 修正有效去除了扭折平均后 AFQMC 在体硅及哑铃、六角自间隙中残留的两体有限尺寸误差。修正后，16 原子胞中两种自间隙的形成能为 4.4 eV 和 4.6 eV，与 DMC 及跨关联耦合簇（transcorrelated coupled-cluster）结果一致。四面体自间隙与 Jahn-Teller 空位需 64 原子胞，分别得到 5.7 eV 和 5.2 eV。相同设置下 CCSD(T) 在 16 原子胞中给出系统性偏低的形成能，最大差异（四面体自间隙）为 250 meV。

Conclusion: 此前 CCSD(T) 对四面体自间隙形成能的高估，来源于耦合簇流程中的有限尺寸处理方式，而非缺陷的多组态特性；AFQMC 结合 RPA 有限尺寸修正可为硅点缺陷提供与 DMC 一致的高精度参考形成能。

Abstract: Accurately predicting the energetics of point defects remains a formidable challenge in computational materials science. For the tetrahedral silicon self-interstitial, for example, previous periodic coupled-cluster [CCSD(T)] and diffusion Monte Carlo (DMC) calculations disagree by about 1.2 eV. Here, we present formation energies of silicon self-interstitials and vacancies using auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo (AFQMC) in a plane-wave basis formulated within the projector-augmented wave framework, and compare them with the random-phase approximation (RPA) and CCSD(T). Using 16- and 64-atom cells, we demonstrate that an RPA-based correction effectively removes the two-body finite-size errors that remain after twist averaging in AFQMC for bulk silicon and for the dumbbell and hexagonal self-interstitials. With this correction, we obtain formation energies of 4.4 eV and 4.6 eV for these two self-interstitials in 16-atom cells, in good agreement with DMC and transcorrelated coupled-cluster results. For the tetrahedral interstitial and the Jahn--Teller-distorted vacancy, 16-atom cells are too small to obtain realistic formation energies; in 64-atom cells, we obtain 5.7 eV and 5.2 eV, respectively. With identical setups in 16-atom cells, CCSD(T) yields systematically lower formation energies than AFQMC, with the largest difference, 250 meV, for the tetrahedral interstitial. Our results indicate that the earlier overestimation of CCSD(T) for the tetrahedral interstitial stems from the finite-size treatment in the coupled-cluster workflow rather than from multi-configurational character of the defect.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Structural symmetry, thermodynamic phase stability, and elastic-wave response provide related but distinct descriptions of a transforming solid. We formulate a symmetry-stratified framework that connects these descriptions through a finite-dimensional internal state and a parent-invariant free energy. Stabilizers classify structural states, whereas free-energy comparisons and stability conditions determine equilibrium branches and dissipative evolution. Equivariance ensures that the frozen elastic tangent inherits the structural stabilizer. Equality requires additional identifiability conditions. Little-group representations and degenerate perturbation theory then determine whether an acoustic doublet splits and the first nonzero splitting order $p$. On a selected continuous branch with order-parameter exponent $β$, a regular, observable acoustic gap scales as $|t|^{pβ}$, provided critical self-energies and structural relaxation do not dominate the measurement. Explicit tensorial and cubic-to-tetragonal realizations yield linear and quadratic splitting channels, together with directionally protected degeneracies. Separate numerical benchmarks examine free-energy reconstruction, coexistence, elastic compatibility, finite-temperature spectra, and controlled critical readouts. Reanalysis of NiTi measurements illustrates effective elastic degeneracy, thermal-history dependence, and the predictive value of independently measured phase fractions. Most importantly, the framework identifies the constitutive and measurement assumptions required to infer structural changes from mechanical spectra.

</details>

Scanning electron microscopy and energy-dispersive spectroscopy show a Yb:Mo ratio close to the expected 2:1 stoichiometry. Optical measurements based on diffuse-reflectance spectra and Tauc analysis yield an estimated optical gap of $\sim 3.28$ eV under the direct-allowed transition assumption. Magnetic susceptibility measurements reveal Curie-Weiss-like paramagnetic behavior, with an effective magnetic moment of $μ_{\mathrm{eff}} \approx 4.2μ_B$ per Yb and an apparent Weiss temperature of $θ\approx -65.7$ K, consistent with possible antiferromagnetic interactions or correlations, with no clear magnetic-ordering transition observed down to 10 K. Abstract: In this paper we present a systematic first-principles investigation of the structure and nearest-neighbor magnetic exchange interactions in the infinite-layer RNiO2 thin-film series (R = La-Lu), under the constrain of different substrates. Abstract: Hematite is an insulating iron oxide with both a collinear antiferromagnetic and a weakly ferromagnetic phase with intriguing properties, such as long-range spin transport distances, that make it promising for spintronics applications. We show that dipolar interactions lead to a sizeable non-altermagnetic splitting and a hybridisation between opposite-chirality magnons resulting in a strong reduction of the spin expectation value of magnons close to the Γ point and around nodal planes, even in the collinear out-of-plane phase. Both the frequencies and the lifetimes have a large altermagnetic splitting in the $k_z = 0$ plane, which is characteristic of hematite and not generally expected for g-wave altermagnets. We compare our results to the prototypical, hexagonal g-wave altermagnet manganese telluride, which has four symmetry-protected nodal planes, whereas the fourth nodal surface in hematite is not completely fixed by symmetry. We derive implicit analytical expressions for this surface and discuss how it arises from a combination of rotational and translational symmetry, which leads to a transition to i-wave symmetry at the Brillouin zone boundary. The example of hematite reveals the trigonal altermagnets as a special case where the splitting function exhibits both bulk (g-wave) and planar (i-wave) components, and shows that the point symmetries alone are not sufficient to capture the full symmetry-determined behaviour of the splitting function.

Abstract: Metal halide perovskites have spin-orbit coupling among the strongest in semiconductors, yet whether their centrosymmetric bulk phases can convert charge current into spin current, and what controls the response, is unknown. Here we identify, from first-principles calculations on the four all-inorganic compounds CsBX3 (B = Pb, Sn; X = Br, I), the factors that govern their intrinsic spin Hall conductivity. The conductivity arises from the spin Berry curvature of the spin-orbit-split halide-p bands, 1.1-2.6 eV below the valence band maximum, and exceeds that of III-V and group-IV semiconductors when the Fermi level lies inside them. Because the valence band maximum is an s-p singlet, shifted only in second order, these contributions cancel in the undoped compounds, which respond like conventional semiconductors. With hole doping the response recovers, and at densities reachable by electric double-layer gating CsPbI3 attains the spin Hall conductivity of p-GaAs. The response is therefore determined by the position of the halide-p bands relative to the valence band maximum, not by the atomic spin-orbit constant, a criterion that applies to any semiconductor with an orbital-singlet band edge. These results point to CsPbI3 as a candidate gate-controlled source of spin current for perovskite spin-orbit torque devices.

Abstract: Antiferromagnets with broken time-reversal symmetry, including altermagnets, have attracted growing interest because they can exhibit ferromagnet-like responses, such as the anomalous Hall effect and magneto-optical effects, despite their compensated spin structures. Such time-reversal symmetry breaking is characterized by ferroic time-reversal-odd multipoles, distinct from the conventional spin and orbital moments. Among them, the anisotropic magnetic dipole (AMD), which reflects the local orbital anisotropy, shares the symmetry of magnetization and can have a ferroic component even when the spin moments cancel. AMD responses have thus far been identified primarily in antiferromagnets with unit-cell magnetic periodicity, whereas spatially modulated AMDs in long-wavelength magnetic textures remain unexplored. Here, we report resonant soft x-ray small-angle scattering measurements on the antiferromagnetic spin cycloid of the multiferroic BiFeO$_3$. We observe pronounced circular dichroism in the resonant scattering intensity, i.e., scattering circular dichroism, which is not expected from the established cycloidal and Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya-interaction-induced sinusoidal spin structures. We show that the staggered rotation of the FeO$_6$ octahedra gives rise to ferroic AMD components despite the antiparallel spin alignment. As the local magnetic symmetry varies with the cycloidal phase, these AMDs are spatially modulated and produce the dichroism through interference with the sinusoidal spin component. Our findings demonstrate that long-wavelength antiferromagnetic textures can host spatially modulated AMDs, opening new avenues for exploring hidden multipole textures in magnetic and multiferroic materials.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量催化剂筛选需要快速且准确的吸附能预测，但现有方法要么速度慢（全图机器学习势），要么缺乏几何敏感性（如 alpha-scheme 等基于配位数的方法），无法兼顾速度、几何精度与可解释性。

Conclusion: AdsNet 建立了一个准确、高效且可解释的框架，适用于大规模吸附能预测与跨多种材料表面的局部界面弛豫，为大范围催化剂筛选提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Fast and accurate prediction of adsorption energies is critical for high-throughput computational catalyst screening, existing methods compromise either on evaluation speed (full-graph machine learning potentials) or geometric sensitivity (coordination-based models like the alpha-scheme). Here, we present AdsNet, an equivariant graph neural network that directly predicts adsorption energies in a single forward pass, which operates on a local graph restricted only to adsorbate atoms and its immediate surface neighbors within a cutoff radius. This local graph formulation decouples computational complexity from total system size while encoding continuous geometric features (interatomic distances and directional vectors) enabling sensitivity to subtle variations in local bonding environments. AdsNet adopts a message-passing architecture with scalar and vector node features over multiple interaction layers. Trained on AdsTraj, a dataset of 33,920 density functional theory (DFT) calculated structures spanning 26 transition metals and 6 adsorbates (H*, C*, O*, CO*, OH*, HOCO*), the model achieves an adsorption energy root-mean-square error (RMSE) of 0.032 eV and a force RMSE of 0.047 eV/A. Analytic forces obtained via automatic differentiation enable structure relaxation through a projected gradient descent algorithm with adaptive line search, eliminating entire geometry optimizations. Additionally, AdsNet uniquely decomposes predicted adsorption energies into contributions from individual atoms within the local adsorbate graph - an interpretability feature unattainable with conventional machine-learning potentials. AdsNet thus establishes an accurate, highly efficient, and interpretable framework for large-scale adsorption energy prediction and local interface relaxation across various material surfaces.

Reducing the flake thickness induces multiple stepwise resistance jumps with enhanced hysteresis, corresponding to transitions between stacking domains accompanied by ripplocations. Statistical analysis of the jumps reveals collective avalanche behavior of domain dynamics with universal power-law scaling, quantifying the domain motion. Harnessing these features, we demonstrate controllable, non-volatile multi-level resistive switching, including write/erase operations, via thermal cycling. These findings provide valuable insights into the sliding dynamics for various 2D materials, with significant implications for the development of slidetronics devices.

Abstract: The ability to measure thermal properties and visualize heat transport processes with high spatial resolution is becoming increasingly important for materials discovery, next-generation electronics, and advanced manufacturing. As critical dimensions and architectures of novel materials and high performance electronics shrink and become more heterogeneous, thermal transport is no longer governed by bulk material properties alone, but by local variations in microstructure, such as interfaces and defects, while the operating conditions can also play a role. With the development of non-contact pump-probe thermoreflectance techniques, specific heat, thermal conductivity, and interface conductance can be mapped either through wide-field thermos-reflectance images or by scanning technique. However, key challenges remain such as the balance between imaging throughput and measurement accuracy, the extraction of multiple parameters with high confidence, and non-intrusive subsurface characterization, etc. This Review highlights recent advancements in artificial intelligence (AI)-assistance in high-throughput data processing, visualizing interfacial thermal transport, and nonintrusive subsurface analysis, and offers outlooks for future research directions such as spectral mapping of heat carriers, three-dimensional property mapping, and AI-agentic experimental designs. The crystallization process was characterized through the temperature dependence of the potential energy. Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 实验测量由振荡磁偶极子产生并经金属板反射的低频磁场横向分量的大小和相位；该量完全由横电（s）偏振的隐失波决定，并与采用标准 Drude 响应函数的理论预言进行对比。

Result: 测量数据与基于标准 Drude 响应函数的理论预言存在定量与定性上的不一致，表明在低频 s 偏振隐失波区域，Drude 响应函数被实验数据排除。

Conclusion: 由于 s 偏振隐失波正是导致卡西米尔之谜的原因，该结果揭示了谜团的成因；同时强调寻找金属对隐失波的适当非局域响应函数不仅对卡西米尔效应，而且对近场光学显微、纳米光子学、表面等离激元、辐射传热等领域都至关重要。

Abstract: We report a high-precision experiment on measuring the low-frequency magnetic field reflected from a metallic plate. This experiment is designed to give insight into why theoretical predictions of the fundamental Lifshitz theory using the standard response function to the electromagnetic waves are in contradiction with the data of numerous precision measurements of the Casimir force (the Casimir puzzle). To attain these ends, we measured the magnitude and phase of the lateral component of the magnetic field produced by an oscillating magnetic dipole and reflected from a metallic plate. These quantities are completely determined by the evanescent waves with the transverse electric (s) polarization. The obtained measurement data are compared with theoretical predictions using the standard Drude response function of the metal to the low-frequency electromagnetic waves. It is shown that there are both quantitative and qualitative disagreements with these predictions, so that in the region of low-frequency s-polarized evanescent waves the Drude response function is excluded by the data. Keeping in mind that just the s-polarized evanescent waves are responsible for the Casimir puzzle, our results highlight the causes for its emergence. It is emphasized that, in addition to the Casimir effect, searching for the adequate nonlocal response functions of metals to the evanescent waves is of utmost significance for many areas of physics as near-field optical microscopy, nanophotonics, surface plasmon polaritons, radiative heat transfer, and others.

</details>

*Paul Werner,Wiebke Bennecke,Jan Philipp Bange,Junde Liu,Daniel Steil,Marcel Reutzel,G. S. Matthijs Jansen,Stefan Mathias*

TL;DR: 本文介绍 momipy，一个用于光电子动量显微术（动量显微成像）数据高效处理的 Python 软件包，支持交互式 4D 可视化、能量/动量标定、空间电荷校正及多维数据拟合等功能。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动量显微术的时间分辨测量产生高维数据（电子面内动量 kx、ky，动能 Ekin，时间 Δt 等），其快速采集给数据处理与分析带来巨大挑战。典型工作流涉及数据采集、探索性可视化、标定与特征提取，且数据管理需遵循 FAIR 原则，因此需要一个高效、低计算成本的通用分析工具。

Method: 作者开发了 momipy Python 软件包，整合了动量显微术数据分析工作流的核心环节，包括交互式 4D 数据可视化、能量与动量标定、空间电荷校正，以及分箱（binning）、动量图提取和基于时间/能量/动量分辨的模型拟合等多维数据分析算法；同时提供配套的交互式示例脚本以降低使用门槛。

Result: momipy 能够以低计算成本高效地完成动量显微术数据的处理与分析，覆盖从高维数据可视化、标定、校正到多维拟合的完整流程，并且借助交互式示例脚本使初学者和经验丰富的研究者都能快速上手。

Conclusion: momipy 为动量显微术社区提供了一个高效、易用且符合 FAIR 数据管理理念的 Python 分析工具，有望推动时间分辨光电子动量显微术的数据处理标准化与应用普及。

Abstract: Photoemission momentum microscopy is an emerging approach to photoelectron spectroscopy that is transforming electronic band structure mapping at both laboratory-based and large-scale facilities. Its extension to time-resolved measurements has recently enabled major advances, ranging from the femtosecond imaging of exciton wavefunctions to dark-field photoelectron microscopy of light-induced dynamics and strong light-matter interactions. However, the rapid acquisition of high-dimensional data sets (including electron in-plane momenta $k_x$ and $k_y$, kinetic energy $E_\mathrm{kin}$, time $Δt$, and more) poses substantial challenges for data handling and analysis. A typical workflow encompasses data acquisition, exploratory visualization, calibration and targeted feature extraction, with data management ideally guided by FAIR principles. Here, we present momipy, a Python package that streamlines the momentum microscopy data analysis workflow efficiently and at low computational cost. Among other features, momipy provides interactive 4D data visualization, energy and momentum calibration, space-charge correction, and a broad selection of multidimensional data analysis routines, including binning, momentum-map extraction and time-, energy- and momentum-resolved model fitting. momipy is accompanied by a selection of interactive example scripts, supporting adoption of momipy by both experienced researchers and users new to momentum microscopy.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The martensitic transformation and associated tensile strain of $\mathrm{Ni}_{50}\mathrm{Fe}_{23}\mathrm{Ga}_{27}$ microwires produced by the Taylor--Ulitovsky method were investigated using thermomechanical analysis, electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD), and in-situ tensile X-ray diffraction (XRD). EBSD mapping at room temperature reveals cubic austenite in a nonstrained part of the microwire and tetragonal martensite in the strained part. Both EBSD and XRD show that both austenite and martensite have $[001]$ directions approximately parallel to the wire axis. The axial transformation strain calculated from the measured lattice parameters is approximately $15.4\%$. Thermal cycling under constant stresses of $20\text{--}100~\mathrm{MPa}$ reveals experimental transformation strains of $13.2\text{--}14.0\%$. Increasing tensile stress raises the transformation temperatures, with a stress--temperature slope of approximately $1.52~\mathrm{MPa}/^\circ\mathrm{C}$, comparable to that reported for $[001]$-oriented $\mathrm{Ni\text{--}Fe\text{--}Ga}$ single crystals. Overall, the observations confirm that the large macroscopic strain of up to $14.0\%$ relates to the nearly complete martensitic transformation in the microwire.

*Simon Fernbach,Lisa Mitterhuber,Egbert Zojer,Natalia Bedoya-Martínez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究现代HEMT设计中薄膜层的热输运，采用三种复杂度递增的声子输运模型（从德拜近似到DFT第一性原理框架），并结合合金散射及晶界/膜界散射，用TDTR实测数据拟合，发现第一性原理模型性能最佳且物理一致性最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确描述HEMT设计中薄膜层的热输运特性对于器件热管理至关重要，但现有模型在精度与物理自洽性上存在不足，需要系统评估不同声子输运模型的准确性与预测能力，特别是建立可靠地确定由Umklapp散射主导的本征输运性质的方法。

Conclusion: 第一性原理框架结合DFT声子性质是描述HEMT薄膜热输运的最优方法；通过先确定Umklapp散射主导的本征性质、再系统添加额外散射项的策略，可有效评估并提升模型的准确性与预测能力。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate thermal transport in a set of thin-film layers used in modern HEMT designs. Our theoretical analysis employs three phonon-transport models of increasing sophistication, ranging from a relatively simple Debye-approximation model to a first-principles framework that incorporates Density Functional Theory (DFT)-derived harmonic and anharmonic phonon properties. To describe ternary semiconductors, alloy scattering is also considered, and to account for the properties of non-ideal, polycrystalline thin films, scattering at grain and film boundaries is taken into account. For modeling the specific samples at hand, the latter are fitted to thermal conductivity data measured with Time-Domain Thermoreflectance (TDTR). Overall, we find that the first-principles model performs best and produces the most physically consistent results. Special emphasis is placed on establishing a reliable procedure for determining intrinsic transport properties dominated by Umklapp scattering. Building on these, it is then possible to systematically add additional scattering terms to the models and assess how model-inherent factors, such as the treatment of scattering rates or the description of the phonon dispersion, affect model accuracy and overall predictive capabilities.

### [57] [Alloying effects on the interface cohesion of grain boundaries in the Ni--Fe--Cr system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25944)
*Aljoscha Felix Baumann,Reyhaneh Ghassemizadeh,Daniel F. Urban*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一条结合DFT与机器学习原子间势(MLIP)的工作流,用于量化Fe、Cr等合金元素对Ni基多晶合金晶界(GB)结合力的影响,并将结果用于多尺度断裂建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多晶Ni基合金中晶界强烈影响力学强度和断裂行为,但Fe、Cr等合金元素对晶界结合力的影响难以系统量化;纯DFT计算受系统尺寸和构型采样限制,难以在精细浓度步长下系统考察合金化效应。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论(DFT)计算六个对称倾转Ni晶界的刚体分离(RBS)曲线,通过普适结合能关系(UBER)提取分离功$W_{\mathrm{sep}}$和最大牵引力$σ_{\max}$;将多种机器学习原子间势(MLIP)基础模型与DFT数据集对比,筛选出与DFT定量一致的模型;利用该MLIP在更细浓度步长下系统采样合金化构型;并将二元趋势推广到接近商用Alloy 718主成分的Ni₅₆Fe₂₀Cr₂₄三元合金。

Result: 在晶界平面内以Cr或Fe取代Ni可使$W_{\mathrm{sep}}$与$σ_{\max}$相对于纯Ni晶界均增大;筛选出的MLIP在这两项指标上与DFT定量吻合,并能支持系统采样;合金化引起的晶界结合力变化同时取决于元素类型和局部结构,揭示出仅用DFT无法获得的趋势;二元趋势可作为解释Fe与Cr在Ni₅₆Fe₂₀Cr₂₄三元体系中协同效应的有用基础,但需显式三元计算才能定量刻画其相互作用。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一套经验证的量化合金元素对多组元合金晶界结合力影响的工作流,所得数值可作为连续尺度断裂模型的输入,为原子信息驱动的多尺度建模奠定基础。

Abstract: Grain boundaries (GBs) in polycrystalline Ni-based alloys strongly influence mechanical strength and fracture behavior, yet the effect of alloying elements such as Fe and Cr on GB cohesion remains difficult to quantify systematically. Using density functional theory (DFT), we compute rigid-body separation (RBS) curves for six symmetric-tilt Ni GBs, extracting the work of separation $W_{\mathrm{sep}}$ and maximum traction $σ_{\max}$ via the universal binding energy relation (UBER). We find that substitution of Cr or Fe into the GB plane increases both quantities relative to pure Ni GBs. To overcome the size limitations of DFT, we compare several machine-learning interatomic potential (MLIP) foundation models against the DFT dataset and identify a model achieving quantitative agreement with DFT for $W_{\mathrm{sep}}$ and $σ_{\max}$. This MLIP enables systematic sampling of alloying configurations at finer concentration steps. We show that alloying-induced changes in GB cohesion depend on both, element type and local structures, revealing trends that are inaccessible with DFT alone. Extending the analysis to a ternary $\mathrm{Ni}_{56}\mathrm{Fe}_{20}\mathrm{Cr}_{24}$ composition, inspired by the principal constituents of the widely used commercial Alloy 718, shows that the binary trends provide a useful basis for interpreting the combined effect of both substitutes, while explicit ternary calculations are required to capture their interplay quantitatively. Together, these results provide a validated workflow for quantifying the effect of alloying elements on GB cohesion in multicomponent alloys. The resulting values may serve as input for continuum-scale fracture models, paving the way toward atomistically informed multiscale modeling.

The force constants for the lower frequency Raman modes (E1 g and A11g) remain almost composition-independent, while for the higher-frequency ones a profound softening (E2g) or stiffening (A21g) was determined. From the Raman spectroscopy measurements under high-pressure conditions, the corresponding Grüneisen parameters for the three observed Raman-active modes (A11g, E2g, A21g) as a function of composition were determined by combining them with the previously reported Equation of States and the corresponding bulk modulus B0 of the ambient-conditions R-3m phase. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励偏差通常被视为AI的失败；但作者认为在材料发现中，预定义奖励与专家判断之间的分歧恰恰可能揭示被奖励函数忽略的有价值材料。作者希望利用这种系统性的分歧（而非噪声）来发现超出固定奖励设计目标的新材料。

Method: 以电子平带为例：首先比较专家判断与数值带宽分数，确认专家分歧是系统性的（无法用约5%的错误标签率解释）；随后通过成对比较学习专家偏好，并将学到的偏好用于引导晶体扩散模型（crystal diffusion model）进行材料生成；最后将框架扩展到可合成性与声子性质任务。

Result: 学到的专家偏好与数值分数在最平坦的能带处一致，但在其他区域分歧明显。专家偏好发现了一些数值分数会舍弃的已报道化合物，如具有部分平带的KCo2As2和CoGa。专家与数值评分的分歧是系统性的，而非由约5%的标注错误导致。

Conclusion: 奖励偏差可以成为材料发现中的机遇来源，帮助揭示超出固定奖励预设目标之外的材料，从而拓展生成式AI在材料科学中的发现能力。

Abstract: Reward misalignment is usually regarded as a failure of artificial intelligence. In materials discovery with generative AI models, however, disagreement between predefined rewards and expert judgment may reveal materials that the rewards overlook. We investigate this possibility using electronic flat bands as a prime example. Experts mostly agree with a numerical bandwidth score, but their disagreements are systematic and cannot be explained by an estimated 5% rate of mistaken labels. We learn the experts' preferences from pairwise comparisons and use them to guide a crystal diffusion model. The learned preferences and numerical score agree on the flattest bands but diverge elsewhere. For example, the preferences favor KCo2As2 and CoGa, reported compounds with partially flat bands that the score would discard. The framework also extends to synthesizability and phonon properties. Our findings suggest that reward misalignment can expose opportunities for materials discovery beyond what fixed rewards are designed to find.

</details>


### [61] [Unusual increase in electrical and thermal conductivity of severely deformed CrMnFeCoNi high-entropy alloy: Roles of disordering and nanostructuring in electron and phonon transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26213)
*Jacqueline Hidalgo-Jiménez,Payam Edalati,Md Amirul Islam,Makoto Arita,Dmitry Chernyshov,Bidyut Baran Saha,Kaveh Edalati*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在CrMnFeCoNi高熵合金中，高压扭转(HPT)引起的严重塑性变形虽提升硬度，却出乎意料地同时提高了电导率和热导率(热导率提升约40%)。分析表明电子传输仅小幅提升(~5%)，而声子传输几乎翻倍(~100%)，且热输运接近最低热导率极限，归因于变形诱导的短程有序等原子尺度无序的调控，而非传统缺陷散射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究缺陷工程和严重塑性变形诱导的纳米结构化如何影响高熵合金CrMnFeCoNi的电输运和热输运特性，揭示缺陷、原子尺度无序与纳米结构在调控电子和声子散射中的复杂相互作用。

Method: 采用高压扭转(HPT)进行最多N=4圈的严重塑性变形，形成分级缺陷结构(低应变变形孪晶、中应变位错与Lomer-Cottrell锁、大应变高比例大角晶界)。通过硬度、电导率、热导率、比热容和线性热膨胀测量，结合Wiedemann-Franz定律分离电子与声子贡献，并与Cahill-Pohl最低热导率模型对比分析声子输运机制。

Conclusion: HPT虽增强缺陷散射，却反常提升电导与热导，表明热输运不受传统缺陷散射控制，而是由变形诱导短程有序等原子尺度无序的调控所致。研究凸显了严重变形高熵合金中缺陷、原子尺度无序与纳米结构对电热性能的复杂协同作用。

Abstract: This work investigates how defect engineering and nanostructuring via severe plastic deformation influence electrical and thermal transport in the high-entropy alloy CrMnFeCoNi. High-pressure torsion (HPT) for up to N = 4 turns produces a hierarchical defect structure, including deformation twins at low strains, dislocations and Lomer-Cottrell locks at intermediate strains, and a high fraction of high-angle grain boundaries at large strains. While hardness naturally increases and saturates at ~520 Hv, both electrical and thermal conductivity exhibit an unusual increase upon HPT processing (from 0.953 MS m-1 and 10.3 W m-1 K-1 after homogenization to 1.003 +- 0.005 MS m-1 and 14.4 W m-1 K-1 after N = 4). Analysis based on the Wiedemann-Franz law indicates a modest increase in electron transport (~5%) and a significant increase in phonon transport (~100%) after HPT processing. The phonon mean free path remains small on the order of 0.6-1.1 nm, and comparison with the Cahill-Pohl model shows that heat transport occurs near the minimum thermal conductivity limit, where vibrational modes are strongly scattered. This suggests that the enhancement in thermal transport is not governed by conventional defect scattering, but it is hypothesized that modifications in atomic-scale disorder, such as deformation-induced short-range ordering, are responsible for this unusual behavior. Specific heat capacity increases after HPT, consistent with stored defect energy. The linear thermal expansion exhibits a non-monotonic behavior, decreasing initially due to internal stresses and relaxation effects, and increasing at higher strains due to grain boundary-induced anharmonicity. These findings highlight the complex interplay between defects, atomic-scale disorder, and nanostructure in governing the electrical and thermal properties of severely deformed high-entropy alloys via electron and phonon scattering.

</details>

Conclusion: Perfumer 表明，将大语言模型智能体与机器学习预测模型同第一性原理代码结合，可在保持精度的同时显著加速结构弛豫与能带预测，并以自然语言接口降低使用门槛，为高通量材料性质预测提供了一条高效可行的路径。

Abstract: First-principles structural relaxation and band calculations require substantial computation and expertise. Here we present Perfumer, a large language model-based agentic framework that couples Quantum ESPRESSO with two predictive models. Across tested structures, pre-relaxation with Perfumer-relax model followed by QE refinement yielded a 2.75-fold overall speedup over QE alone. Perfumer-band model combines a periodic graph neural network with a latent Hermitian spectral head returns 12 band energies at any requested wavevector, preserving reciprocal lattice periodicity and time-reversal symmetry without constructing Hamiltonian. Trained on 125,153 self-curated band structures, Perfumer-band model reaches a median per-material mean absolute error of 0.157 eV on 2,607 test materials, including lanthanide, actinide and magnetic compounds, from held-out composition families spanning 85 elements, taking about 97 ms per material on two consumer CPU cores. The agent turns natural-language requests into relaxation-to-band workflows, from a single uploaded structure to automatically constructed new candidates and returns predicted properties.

Abstract: The terahertz resonance frequencies of antiferromagnets characterize their precessional dynamics but do not necessarily imply faster laser-induced quenching of magnetic order. Here, we investigate the distinct roles of magnetic compensation and electronic scattering phase space by comparing ultrafast demagnetization in the antiferromagnetic charge-transfer insulator NiO, the metallic antiferromagnet Mn2Au, and a ferromagnetic Ni reference. The extracted response in Mn2Au is approximately four times faster than in NiO and substantially faster than in Ni. These observations are consistent with exchange-mediated scattering between antiparallel sublattices enabling rapid loss of antiferromagnetic order without requiring net angular-momentum transfer to an external reservoir, provided sufficient electronic scattering phase space is available. In insulating NiO, restricted electronic scattering channels reduces the ultrafast response. Our results distinguish ultrafast demagnetization from exchange-enhanced precession and identify the interplay of spin-flip probability and electronic scattering rate as a key factor governing the nonequilibrium response of compensated magnets.

### [64] [Constitutive Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks in Finite Element Model Updating: Closed-Form Hyperelastic Strain Energies from Heterogeneous Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26528)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超弹性本构模型标定存在两大难题：需专家预先选定函数形式，且需多个均匀实验确定参数。神经本构模型解决了前者，但要么是黑箱，要么依赖预先固定的候选项库。全场测量（如DIC）可解决后者，但仍有两点空白：训练时灵活、训练后可解释，以及仅用单个非均匀实验训练模型的能力。

Method: 将本构柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络（CKAN）嵌入有限元模型更新（FEMU），通过最小化预测与实测的运动学场和反力之间的失配来训练网络；随后用剪枝和符号回归将训练后的网络化简为紧凑、满足全部本构要求的闭式应变能表达式。

Result: 在多孔天然橡胶膜上，CKAN复现DIC位移场的中位误差在1%~2%之间，并能迁移到训练中未见过的孔型；在多孔硅胶板上，在单一载荷下以一个拉伸剖面训练的CKAN能以百分之几的误差复现该剖面及实测反力；在加压膨胀的主动脉试样上，各向异性CKAN能预测三维膨胀形状；发现的闭式能量在训练数据上与所替代的CKAN匹配，并可直接与成熟模型比较。

Conclusion: 该方法同时解决了神经本构模型的可解释性缺失与标定需多个均匀实验两大问题，实现了在单个非均匀实验上训练兼具可解释性的本构模型，并通过三个实验（橡胶膜、硅胶板、主动脉）验证了其准确性、迁移性与闭式可解释性。

Abstract: Calibrating a hyperelastic constitutive model for a soft material traditionally poses two problems: an expert must choose its functional form, and several homogeneous tests must determine its parameters. Neural constitutive models solve the first, but either remain black boxes or draw on a library of candidate terms fixed in advance. Full-field measurements, such as those from digital image correlation (DIC), promise to solve the second, replacing those tests by a single heterogeneous one. Two gaps remain: flexibility during training with interpretability afterwards, and a way to train such a model on one heterogeneous test. Here we close both. We embed a constitutive Kolmogorov-Arnold network (CKAN) in finite element model updating (FEMU) and train it by minimizing the mismatch between the predicted and the measured kinematic fields and reaction forces. Pruning and symbolic regression then reduce the trained network to a compact closed-form strain energy that satisfies every constitutive requirement. On perforated natural-rubber membranes, the CKANs reproduce the DIC displacement fields with median errors between one and two percent, and transfer to a hole pattern withheld from training. On a perforated silicone plate, a CKAN trained on one stretch profile at a single load reproduces that profile and the measured reaction force to within a few percent. On an aortic specimen inflated under pressure, an anisotropic CKAN predicts the inflated three-dimensional shape. The discovered closed-form energies match the CKANs they replace on the training data and can be compared directly with established models.

</details>


### [65] [Emergent Fatigue Dynamics from Foundational Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26533)
*Jingye Tan,Benjamin A. Jasperson,Eric Rothchild,Anders Johansson,Rèmi Dingreville,Krishna Garikipati,Mitchell A. Wood*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 基础机器学习原子间势在近平衡性质上与EAM势一致，但因预测异常低的层错能而在疲劳载荷下产生截然不同的塑性行为，提示仅靠平衡态验证不足以保证大尺度分子动力学的涌现行为正确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础机器学习原子间势虽在大规模DFT数据集上训练，但其验证通常局限于平衡态，无法保证在需要外部疲劳应力驱动稀有事件的大尺度分子动力学中涌现出正确的行为；在缺乏直接实验验证的情况下，需要与物理动机明确的经典势进行对比以获取洞察。

Method: 区分热可达的静态性质与需外部疲劳应力驱动稀有事件的涌现性质，对EAM势与NequIP基础势在两类性质上分别进行对比；通过疲劳循环加载模拟经典Griffith裂纹问题的纳米级缺陷，并分析加载后每原子能量分布；同时记录了实际使用中的内存相关崩溃与静默外推问题。

Result: 两种势在多数近平衡性质上与参考数据高度一致，但NequIP预测的内禀层错能异常偏低（约为EAM的1/7），导致其在疲劳循环下预测的塑性变形远多于EAM；加载后的每原子能量分布显示两者在远高于热可达能量的区域差异最大。

Conclusion: 仅靠平衡态验证无法暴露基础势在稀有事件驱动的涌现行为上的缺陷，层错能等关键材料参数的准确性对疲劳等非平衡过程的预测至关重要；同时提醒从业者注意该类基础模型在实际使用中的内存相关崩溃与静默外推等工程问题。

Abstract: Foundational machine-learned interatomic potentials are trained on large, diverse DFT datasets, but their validation rests on equilibrium states, which does not guarantee correct emergent behavior in large-scale molecular dynamics. Where no direct experimental validation is available, comparisons to physically motivated potentials are valuable for drawing insight. We distinguish static properties, which are thermally accessible, from emergent properties, which require work from an external fatigue stress to drive rare events, and compare the potentials on both. An EAM potential and the NequIP foundational potential both agree closely with reference data for many near-equilibrium properties, but NequIP predicts an anomalously low intrinsic stacking fault energy ($\sim7$ times lower than EAM) and thus predicts far more plastic deformation under fatigue cycling of a nanoscale flaw modeled after the classical Griffith crack problem. Per-atom energy distributions after loading show that the two potentials differ most at energies far above what is thermally accessible. We also document memory-related crashes and silent extrapolation as practical concerns for practitioners of these foundational model forms.

</details>

### [66] [Where electrons conduct and where to change it: gauge-invariant sensitivities and the networks of sensitive bonds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25104)
*D A Drabold,C Ugwumadu*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文解决“无序固体中电导对局部扰动的响应发生在何处”这一精确问题，给出Kubo-Greenwood电导对哈密顿量的规范不变导数，并在非晶硅上验证，发现最敏感键形成树状细丝和环状网络，与现有电导分配方法（SPC、KuboMap、LDOS）不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分两个不同问题：电流在无序固体中“在哪里传导”与“在哪里局部改变会改变电流”。前者已有多种分配方法，后者（电导对局部扰动的响应）缺少精确、规范不变的理论构造。

Method: 给出Kubo-Greenwood电导对哈密顿量的规范不变导数作为对任意局部变化的响应；在非晶硅上应用紧束缚模型（最多10000原子）和密度泛函模型（最多512原子），并与空间投影电导（SPC）、KuboMap和局域态密度（LDOS）三种电导分配方式对比。

Result: KuboMap与LDOS高度相关（中位Spearman相关0.97）；而敏感性图不同：它能准确排序对在位能移动的响应，但对移除原子的排序差，跨哈密顿量迁移性也较差。最敏感键形成连通团簇：价带尾为24–38原子的树状细丝，带隙深处为环状网络；相对LDOS加权随机选择超额达48个标准差，且仅在展宽约跨越五个能级间距时出现。细丝与尾态本征态及主导传导通道重叠远超随机团簇，但无单一态或通道可解释；这些网络在300 K热快照中大部分消失。

Conclusion: “电导在哪里承载”与“电导在哪里响应”是两个不同问题：局部输运图对前者有用，对后者作用有限。精确的敏感性构造揭示出局域化细丝/网络结构，且这些结构对温度和模型细节敏感。

Abstract: Where is the electrical current carried in a disordered solid, and where would a local change alter it? The two questions differ. We answer the second exactly: the derivative of the Kubo-Greenwood conductivity with respect to the Hamiltonian is gauge invariant and gives the response to any local change. The construction applies to any Hamiltonian. We try it on amorphous silicon, with tight-binding cells of up to 10000 atoms and density-functional cells of up to 512 atoms, and compare it with three ways of allocating the conductivity: the space-projected conductivity (SPC), KuboMap, and the local density of states (LDOS). KuboMap follows the LDOS closely (median Spearman correlation 0.97). The sensitivity does not. It ranks the response to an on-site shift accurately and the response to removing an atom poorly, and it transfers between Hamiltonians less well than the other maps. The most sensitive bonds form connected clusters. In three large networks they are tree-like filaments of 24 to 38 atoms in the valence tail and looped networks deeper in the gap; the excess over an LDOS-weighted random selection reaches 48 standard deviations and appears only when the broadening spans about five level spacings. The filament overlaps the tail eigenstate with the most weight on it, and one of the leading conduction channels, far more than a random cluster does, but no single state or channel explains it. The networks are largely lost in thermal snapshots at 300 K. Where the conductivity is carried and where it responds are different problems: local transport maps are useful for the first and limited for the second.

</details>


### [67] [Onset of subdiffusion in dephased quasiperiodic lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26082)
*Ashlin V. Thomas,Sanchayan Banerjee,Tapan Mishra*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 研究表明，在具有奇异在位势或消失跃迁的准周期晶格中，退相干/退相位不仅不会导致普通扩散，反而会产生亚扩散输运，并出现扩散—亚扩散转变以及一个“亚扩散沟槽”现象；输运指数具有普适性，仅由准周期调制的奇异性（零点）阶数决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为退相位会使准周期晶格中的输运趋于扩散，但在包含奇异在位势或零跃迁的系统中这一预期可能失效。作者意在揭示此类“本应平庸局域化”的晶格在退相干条件下出现的非平庸输运行为及其普适规律。

Method: 结合解析推导与数值分析，研究奇异（对角项零点）和零跃迁（非对角项零点）准周期调制晶格在退相位下的输运；通过计算晶格“平均电阻”的发散性判定亚扩散条件，并用精确 Lindblad 模拟验证结论。

Result: 发现当晶格的平均电阻发散时，输运可由扩散转变为亚扩散；输运最慢处并不在亚扩散区的深处，而恰好在转变起始点，形成尖锐的“亚扩散沟槽”。输运指数具有普适性，仅由对角（非对角）准周期调制的奇异性（零点）阶数唯一确定。

Conclusion: 退相位在奇异准周期晶格中可引发亚扩散输运而非普通扩散，并伴随扩散—亚扩散转变与“亚扩散沟槽”现象；输运指数普适地由调制奇异性阶数决定。作者通过精确 Lindblad 模拟佐证了上述发现，并给出了实验实现此类非平庸输运的可能途径。

Abstract: Dephasing is widely expected to induce diffusion in quasiperiodic lattices due to the loss of phase coherence. However, this expectation can fail dramatically in lattices possessing singular onsite potentials or vanishing hoppings which are expected to trivially localize the particle. Remarkably, we show that the transport in such lattices can be subdiffusive under proper conditions for which the "mean resistance" of the lattice diverges. As a result, we obtain a transition from diffusive to subdiffusive transport depending on the strength of quasiperiodic modulation. Even more strikingly, the transport is slowest not deep in the subdiffusive regime but precisely at its onset, forming a sharp "subdiffusive trench". Our analytical and numerical analysis reveal that the transport exponents are universal, fixed solely by the order of singularities (zeros) of the diagonal (off-diagonal) quasiperiodic modulation. In the end, we complement our findings through exact Lindblad simulations and provide possible experimental routes to realize such non-trivial transport.

</details>


### [68] [Three-mode chimera-like states in a minimal network of active camphor ribbons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26039)
*Ratan Sarkar,Sapna Yadav,Punit Parmananda,Charo I. del Genio,Stefano Boccaletti*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文在四个相同樟脑椭圆转子系统中实验演示了一种三域类嵌合体（three-mode chimera-like）状态，验证了此前反应扩散模型中三域嵌合体的理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌合体态是同步对称性破缺的显著表现，先前理论工作提出反应扩散模型中存在"三域嵌合体"，但缺乏实验验证。作者旨在用可控物理系统实测这一新型集体动力学态。

Method: 构建四个完全相同的樟脑浸润椭圆转子，分别以方形底座的四个角为支点放置于水面。转子因马兰戈尼驱动流而自推进并旋转，并通过共享水界面实现化学耦合。观测并分析该系统涌现的集体动力学状态。

Result: 系统呈现三种不同的集体动力学状态：同步态（synchronized）、反同步态（antisynchronized）和非相干态（incoherent），从而实验上确认了三模式类嵌合体态的存在。

Conclusion: 实验证实了反应扩散模型中三域嵌合体的理论预测，为非线性动力学提供新见解，并表明这种新型嵌合体可能在更广泛的物理系统中被观测到。

Abstract: Chimera states are a remarkable manifestation of symmetry breaking, wherein identical units spontaneously split into coherent and incoherent groups. The term "chimera" itself is inspired by the mythological creature having parts of different animals, reflecting the coexistence of distinct dynamical behaviors within a single system. Here, we experimentally demonstrate the existence of a three-mode chimera-like state in a system of four identical camphor-infused elliptical rotors, each pivoted at a corner of a square base. When placed on a water surface, the rotors self-propel and rotate due to Marangoni-driven flows. Chemical coupling mediated through the shared water interface gives rise to three distinct collective dynamical states, namely a synchronized one, an antisynchronized one and an incoherent one. Our results validate recent theoretical work about three-domain chimeras in reaction-diffusion models, providing new insights into nonlinear dynamics and suggesting that this new type of chimera may be observed in a broad class of physical systems.

</details>

### [69] [Exact Fractional Chern Insulators from Hubbard Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25010)
*Jonah Herzog-Arbeitman,Rui-Heng Liu,Jiangping Hu,Chen Fang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在紧束缚模型与在位Hubbard相互作用框架下，为分数量子霍尔绝缘体（FCI）构造了多体波函数与其无阻挫父哈密顿量的精确配对，证明FCI基态可存在于理想能带之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Laughlin波函数与Haldane赝势是理解分数量子霍尔效应的精确配对（波函数为无阻挫父哈密顿量的唯一零能基态）。作者希望将这一解析框架推广到分数量子霍尔绝缘体，并突破以往局限于"理想能带"的限制。

Method: 核心方法是引入"朗道对偶陈能带"（Landau-dual Chern band），即在Bravais晶格上直接采样自旋-s狄拉克朗道能级得到的多轨道陈能带。利用矩阵坐标 X+iY+J（其中(X,Y)为晶格坐标，J为自旋空间中的幂零矩阵），解析地写出精确多体波函数，并以在位Hubbard相互作用作为其父哈密顿量。

Result: 构造出的精确配对覆盖了Laughlin、Read-Rezayi和Halperin系列的分数陈绝缘体态，并证明分数量子霍尔绝缘体基态可存在于理想能带之外。

Conclusion: 该工作通过朗道对偶陈能带和矩阵坐标，建立了紧束缚模型中FCI波函数与父哈密顿量的精确解析配对，证明FCI基态存在于理想能带之外，拓展了对分数量子霍尔绝缘体的理解。

Abstract: Laughlin's wavefunction and its parent interaction, Haldane's pseudopotential, underpin our analytic understanding of fractional quantum Hall effect. They are an exact pair in the sense that the wavefunction is the unique, zero-energy ground state of its frustration-free parent Hamiltonian. In this work, we propose exact pairs of many-body wavefunctions for fractional Chern insulators in tight binding models with onsite Hubbard interactions as their parents. The key to our construction is introduction of a Landau-dual Chern band, a multi-orbital Chern band obtained from directly sampling the spin-$s$ Dirac Landau level on a Bravais lattice. The exact many-body wavefunctions are given, analytically, in terms of the matrix coordinate $X+iY+J$, where $(X,Y)$ are the lattice coordinates and $J$ is a nilpotent matrix in spin space. The constructed exact pairs cover the fractional Chern states of the Laughlin, the Read-Rezayi, and the Halperin series. Our results prove that FCI ground states exist beyond ideal bands.

</details>


### [70] [Anyon Coherent States in Fractional Chern Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25034)
*Pedro Fittipaldi de Castro,Haylen Gerhard,Wladimir A. Benalcazar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于算符的系统性方法，用于构造分数量子霍尔态及分数陈绝缘体中任意电荷任意子的最大局域波函数，并揭示这些相干准粒子波函数与任意子能带拓扑、陈数之间的深刻联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分数量子霍尔体系中，相干任意子激发是描述任意子动力学、相互作用和编织的理想对象，但除Laughlin准空穴外，一直缺乏任意子波函数的一般表达式。此外，现有方法难以处理非理想、非涡旋化能带中的分数陈绝缘体。

Method: 作者提出一种基于算符的系统性构造方法，通过局域湮灭算符的零模来得到最大局域化的任意子波函数（可含正负两种电荷），并将其表示为相干准粒子态，其满足投影密度不确定度的饱和界。该方法的关键在于利用任意子能带布洛赫系数的绕数（winding）来编码任意子能带的陈数。

Result: 所构造的波函数是局域湮灭算符的零模，并饱和投影密度不确定度界。这些相干态的存在源于任意子能带的非平庸拓扑；任意子布洛赫系数的绕数即为该任意子能带的陈数。相干态的引导中心可位于晶格内任意位置，且相互重叠按高斯形式衰减，衰减尺度由任意子能带的有效磁长度决定。

Conclusion: 该方法为任意子波函数提供了超越Laughlin准空穴的一般化解析构造，适用于非理想、非涡旋化能带的分数陈绝缘体，并将相干准粒子态、密度不确定度饱和、陈数绕数与高斯重叠衰减等物理性质统一起来，为研究任意子动力学、相互作用与编织提供了新工具。

Abstract: In fractional quantum Hall (FQH) systems, coherent anyonic excitations are the natural entities for describing anyon dynamics, their interactions, and braiding, yet no general expression for their wavefunctions is known beyond Laughlin's quasiholes. We introduce a systematic, operator-based construction of maximally-localized wavefunctions of anyons of either charge in FQH states and fractional Chern insulators, including those in non-ideal, non-vortexable bands. We find that these wavefunctions describe coherent quasiparticles: zero modes of local annihilation operators that saturate projected-density uncertainty bounds. These coherent states owe their existence to a nontrivial topology of the anyon band, with the winding of their Bloch coefficients encoding an anyon band's Chern number. As such, their guiding centers exist anywhere within the lattice, and their mutual overlaps decay as Gaussians determined by the effective magnetic length of the anyon band.

</details>


### [71] [Quantum many-body scars and zero modes in the spin-1 Kitaev chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25051)
*Ayush Sharma,Jay Pandey*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究自旋-1 Kitaev 链，发现其具有手性对称性保护的大量精确零能模式（数量指数增长），并揭示了驱动缺陷动力学的嵌入式紧束缚结构，该结构支持多个此前未报道扇区中的 PXP 型量子多体疤痕。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自旋-1 Kitaev 链的 Hilbert 空间连通性，揭示其手性对称性保护的零能模式数量与结构，并理解其反常的非热化动力学（量子多体疤痕）的起源与机制。

Method: 在笛卡尔基下分析 Hilbert 空间连通性以计数精确零能模式；利用算符-态映射构造具有最大混合约化密度矩阵的解析零模式；识别嵌入式紧束缚结构并运用前向散射近似（FSA）分析 PXP 型量子多体疤痕的动力学。

Result: 偶数长度周期链的零能模式数目下界为 Jacobsthal 数 (2^{L+1}+1)/3；构造出 2^{L/2} 个具有解析表达、对连续二分具有最大混合密度矩阵的零模式；在多个新扇区中识别出支持 PXP 型疤痕的嵌入式紧束缚结构，且 FSA 哈密顿量在不同疤痕扇区中具有普适形式。

Sharp jumps in the torque angular dependence when the field projection along $a$ changes sign are interpreted as field-induced switching between the two degenerate domains with oppositely aligned ferromagnetic moments. We propose a minimal spin Hamiltonian including easy-axis exchange anisotropy and symmetry-allowed Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) couplings, for which mean-field calculations reproduce the main features of the observed angle-dependent torque. Torque measurements up to 35 T applied almost normal to the layers observe a strong anomaly attributed to proximity to the field-induced spin-flop transition predicted for the above Hamiltonian. We contrast the predicted magnetic phase diagrams for magnetic fields in different orthogonal crystallographic planes normal to the honeycomb layers and discuss how DM interactions convert some transitions into smooth crossovers and stabilize a phase boundary line connecting the spin-flop transition in field normal to the layers to magnetization saturation for in-plane field normal to $a$; such a phase boundary line does not exist in the absence of DM interactions.

Abstract: A neural network that learns an inverse map inherits the approximations of the forward solver used to generate its training data. We demonstrate this in the inference of spin Hamiltonians from synthetic neutron-scattering spectra and show how a fast many-body solver can overcome the limitation of linear spin-wave theory (LSWT). We consider a family of anisotropic triangular-lattice spin Hamiltonians at fields just above saturation, where magnon interactions shift and broaden the spectrum in ways that LSWT cannot capture. Using the truncated Hilbert-space ladder (THL) method, we compute the dynamical spin structure factor (DSSF) nonperturbatively within the one- and two-magnon space. THL is fast enough to generate large training sets of DSSF spectra, enabling us to train neural networks that accurately predict Hamiltonian parameters. By empirically benchmarking several neural-network architectures, we find that a one-dimensional convolutional network performs best. A learned forward decoder then reconstructs the DSSF from the predicted parameters, providing a rapid self-consistency check and a visualization of the predicted response. When interactions are strong, networks trained on LSWT and THL spectra infer markedly different parameters. Analysis of the Fisher information matrix shows that the measurement protocol, including which spin-correlation components are resolved and the momentum cut, determines which couplings can be identified. Combining machine learning with fast many- body theory is therefore essential for quantitative Hamiltonian inference from scattering data.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量网络重整化在有限键维 χ 下会引入截断误差，且该误差沿重整化群（RG）流被持续放大。需要精确识别这些误差对应的具体算子及其随 RG 步的生长规律，以理解并控制数值误差。

Result: 施加 O(2) 对称时，主导误差是允许出现的 dimerization（二聚化）算子（因 LoopTNR 破坏单格点平移对称）；对称性较低时主导误差为 U(1) 流或荷为二的顶点算子。二聚化每 RG 步的增长及其振幅的无参数比值与共形微扰论预言在几个百分点内吻合。参数 K 因弱边际误差和二聚化诱导的流动而缓慢漂移。增大 χ 可降低 0.75≤K≤1.5 范围内误差的初始振幅，但不改变主导算子及其增长率。

Conclusion: LoopTNR 在六顶点模型临界线上的截断误差可被精确分解为具体的共形场论算子，其中二聚化算子在 O(2) 对称下主导误差，其增长可由共形微扰论定量解释；增大键维仅降低初值振幅，无法改变误差算子的类型或增长速率。

Abstract: Tensor network renormalization at finite bond dimension $χ$ introduces truncation errors that are amplified by the renormalization-group (RG) flow. For loop tensor network renormalization (LoopTNR) on the $c=1$ critical line of the six-vertex model, with Luttinger parameter $K$, we identify these errors operator by operator from symmetry-resolved transfer-matrix spectra and conformal perturbation theory. With $O(2)$ symmetry imposed, the dominant error is the dimerization operator, which is allowed because LoopTNR breaks one-site translation. With less symmetry it is the $U(1)$ current or a charge-two vertex operator. The growth of the dimerization per RG step and a parameter-free ratio of its amplitudes agree with conformal perturbation theory to within a few percent. The parameter $K$ drifts slowly, owing to a weak marginal error and to the running induced by the dimerization. A larger $χ$ lowers the initial amplitude of the error for $0.75\le K\le 1.5$ but changes neither the operator nor its growth rate.

Abstract: Twisted bilayer graphene hosts strong correlation effects within topological flat bands separated from remote dispersive bands. This separation motivates projection onto the central bands, which greatly reduces the computational complexity of many-body calculations, including momentum-space quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) simulations. However, the interaction-induced Mott gap obtained in the projected model can be comparable to the noninteracting flat--remote band separation, raising the question of whether the projection remains valid. Here, we extend momentum-space QMC to explicitly include remote BM bands within a broad energy window at charge neutrality. We find that the projected description remains reliable over a broad parameter regime, up to $u_0/u_1\lesssim0.9$: the projected and remote-band calculations yield similar low-energy electron and trion spectra, and the Kramers intervalley-coherent ground state remains stable. In particular, the Dirac trion remains robust in the finite-temperature normal state when remote bands are included. Near the band-touching limit $u_0=u_1$, remote-band electrons acquire substantial low-energy spectral weight, accompanied by suppression of the trion weight and loss of the Kramers intervalley-coherent state. Our findings establish the broad validity of flat-band projection while reconciling the projected and THF viewpoints by identifying the near-band-touching regime in which explicit remote-band degrees of freedom become essential.

We identify the two states by the low-energy spectra, particle entanglement spectra, and many-body Chern numbers, using the real-space and band-projected exact diagonalization, supplemented by infinite density-matrix renormalization group calculations. Abstract: Extracting precise universal data on conformal field theory (CFT) on the lattice traditionally requires large-scale simulations of the ground states and excitations of critical systems. Moreover, we show that the Maldacena--Ludwig monopole boundary can be realized as the ground state of the Fidkowski--Kitaev Hamiltonian after space-time rotation.

Conclusion: 通过精确的η二聚体态，为Nagaoka铁磁性提供了更锐利的严格不稳定性边界，厘清了吸引性极化子、二聚子与η二聚体在正方晶格上随填充率变化的竞争图景。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于完全排斥局域双占据的超排斥约束，分别在实空间（SSL 情形）和倒空间（NFL 情形）构建非标准统计描述；同时采用倒空间中类 Hubbard 微观模型进行精确分析，将纯物理论证与微观模型精确解相互印证，进而考察统计转变（如玻色子到费米子的统计嬗变）与关联态的形成条件。

Conclusion: 局域双占据的完全排斥（超排斥）是强关联费米子中非标准统计与关联相形成的核心机制，其作用独立于实空间或倒空间表象；由此可统一理解自旋液体、非费米液体、局域磁矩与 Mott 绝缘态之间的转变，并揭示统计嬗变（玻色子→费米子）在强排斥极限下导致局域化而非凝聚的普适规律。

Abstract: We analyze in detail nonstandard statistics for strongly correlated fermions, which comprise the statistical spin liquid (SSL) and model non-Fermi liquid (NFL) cases. Both of those cases are based on the total exclusion of local doubly occupancies ($the~superexclusion$) either in real space (SSL case) or in reciprocal ($\mathbf{k}$) space (NFL). The results obtained on a purely physical ground are supplemented with the exact analysis of those coming from microscopic Hubbard-like model in $\mathbf{k}$ space. Specifically, we discuss the transmutation of those statistics to that of localized magnetic moments, as well as the transformation of the Fermi liquid into a strongly correlated fermionic liquid or Mott insulating state; the latter taking place for the half-filled-band situation. The role of exclusion of double occupancies in either real or reciprocal space in the appearance of the correlated phase is stressed. Conclusions concerning the universal features of the models, independence of either the real- or $\mathbf{k}$-space representation, are also discussed. One of those is the boson to fermion statistics transmutation which in the limit of strong repulsive interboson interaction leads to the particle localization instead to the Bose-Einstein condensation.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 推导出的eLLG方程中的新项解释了实验上猜想的"激子自旋转移矩"（excitonic STT）的微观起源。该机制表明暗激子可在飞秒激光脉冲关闭后持续向磁振子供给能量，从而解释了磁振子振荡的长时间持续及其高次谐波产生。

Conclusion: 单一微观机制（激子-声子-磁振子耦合产生的类场STT）统一解释了CrSBr中磁振子高次谐波产生与纳秒级持久振荡。暗激子作为能量"储库"为光自旋电子学、光磁子学、神经形态计算及量子换能等领域的工程与应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Recent intense experimental focus on CrSBr, a two-dimensional (2D) antiferromagnetic semiconductor, has revealed numerous surprises in its response to ultrafast optical drive. We demonstrate that a single microscopic mechanism accounts for two of them---high-harmonic generation of magnons and persistence of magnon oscillations over \mbox{$\sim 1$ ns} time scale that is much longer than the femtosecond laser pulse (fsLP) pump duration. This mechanism arises from an unusually complex interplay of four different fundamental quasiparticles of condensed matter physics: magnons, optically bright and dark excitons, and phonons. By viewing magnons as the system and the other three quasiparticles as a quantum many-body bath, we derive an extended Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (eLLG) equation. Its new term generated by the bath is spatially nonlocal and time-dependent, as well as strongly {\em nonlinear} when the {\em crossing of many-body energy levels} of bright and dark excitons occurs via phonon mediation. It also resembles the so-called field-like spin-transfer torque (STT) in spintronics, but STT is driven there with current pulses, so it explains the microscopic origin of the recently experimentally conjectured {\em excitonic STT}. Since dark excitons supply energy to magnons long after the fsLP is switched off, such a reservoir opens avenues for its engineering and applications in optospintronics and optomagnonics, neuromorphic computing, and quantum transduction.

</details>

Abstract: Accurate simulations of strongly correlated electrons remain a central challenge in computational physics. We introduce neural-network Gaussian projected entangled-pair states (NN-GPEPS), which exploit the locally-entangled subspace of Gaussian states to give an architecture more compatible with modern neural networks and an exact contraction cost of $O(N^{3/2})$ on two-dimensional lattices. For the Hubbard model at $U/t=8$ and $1/8$ hole doping, we obtain state-of-the-art variational energies in large lattices of up to 1024 sites. These results demonstrate an accurate approach to strongly correlated fermions by connecting neural networks with Gaussian tensor networks.

</details>

Result: 得到节点 p 波能隙；临界温度在费米面接近磁布里渊区边界时急剧上升，因为 Umklapp 过程中各磁单胞位点在电子-磁振子顶点上的贡献相消更弱，从而增强配对；动量分辨卷绕数预测在对称保持边缘存在零能马约拉纳平带。

Conclusion: 磁振子交换可诱导节点拓扑超导，其配对强度和拓扑性质受费米面与磁布里渊区边界相对位置显著调控，具有可观测的边界马约拉纳平带。

Abstract: We show that magnon exchange can induce nodal topological superconductivity in a microscopic model of a $p$-wave magnet. Solving the weak-coupling multiband gap equation for itinerant electrons exchange-coupled to localized spins, we find a nodal $p$-wave gap and a critical temperature that rises sharply as the Fermi surface approaches the magnetic Brillouin-zone boundary. In this regime, small-reduced-momentum magnons can scatter electrons between Fermi-surface segments on opposite sides of the zone. These Umklapp processes enhance pairing because the contributions from different sites in the magnetic unit cell cancel less in their electron-magnon vertices than in those for ordinary scattering at comparable reduced momenta. Momentum-resolved winding numbers predict zero-energy Majorana flat bands at symmetry-preserving edges where the bulk nodes project to distinct momenta.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Majorana零模与热力学涨落（涡旋-反涡旋配对）耦合后，如何改变传统二维超流/超导体的连续BKT相变性质，并寻找可实现该尺度层级的微观平台与可观测实验信号。

Result: 当Majorana局域长度ξ_F大于涡核尺寸ξ时，邻近涡旋因Majorana杂化获得额外的短程吸引通道，在T_BKT之上产生两涡旋自由能的亚稳极小值；Majorana杂化可驱动相变变为一阶，亚稳相以指数小速率衰变。提出以约瑟夫森耦合的p+ip超导岛阵列为微观实现平台，并预言一阶相变的迟滞回线，其宽度由共存窗口决定。

Abstract: We study the fate of the Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless (BKT) transition in a two-dimensional topological superconductor whose vortex cores each host an isolated Majorana zero mode, energetically well separated from the bulk gap and other bound states of the vortex. When the Majorana localization length $ξ_F$ exceeds the vortex core size $ξ$, nearby vortices acquire an additional short-range attractive channel from Majorana hybridization, modifying the vortex-antivortex interaction at short distances and generating a metastable minimum in the two-vortex free energy above the nominal $T_{BKT}$. We construct an effective field theory for the resulting dilute vortex gas, coupling the ordinary BKT phase field to an additional field encoding the Majorana-mediated interaction, and show, using renormalization-group and instanton methods, that this hybridization can drive the transition first order, with the metastable phase decaying at an exponentially small rate. Finally, we discuss experimental signatures of this first-order transition, in particular a hysteresis loop whose width is set by the coexistence window.

Abstract: To study the influence of magnetic lanthanide (Ln) substituents on La3Ni2O7, polycrystalline samples of La3-xLnxNi2O7 (Ln = Pr, Eu, Gd, Tb, Dy, Ho, Er, Tm, and Yb) were synthesized and characterized by electrical transport, magnetization, and x-ray and neutron powder diffraction before and after high-pressure high-temperature oxygen annealing. The annealing process led to the formation of La3Ni2O7 polymorphs and more metallic-like temperature dependence of the electrical resistance while changing the density-wave transition temperature only slightly. The magnetic signatures were strongly suppressed following oxygen annealing, suggesting a possible association with the accompanying structural modifications. These behaviors distinguish Tb and Dy from Pr, Nd, and Sm substituents which do not introduce magnetic ordering in the system, establishing La3-xTbxNi2O7 and La3-xDyxNi2O7 as promising platforms for exploring the interplay between magnetic order and pressure-induced superconductivity in La3Ni2O7.

### [88] [Generalized Critical-State Analysis of Local Field Screening in High-Pressure Yttrium Polyhydride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25456)
*A. G. Gavriliuk,I. A. Troyan,A. A. Mironovich,A. G. Ivanova,H. -K. Mao,V. V. Struzhkin*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 观测到的磁通排出是钉扎的临界态而非平衡迈斯纳效应；同时展示了一种在极端条件下探测局域超导性的通用方法。

Abstract: We resolve the long-standing controversy over the interpretation of magnetic-flux expulsion in high-pressure superhydrides by measuring the local magnetic field adjacent to yttrium polyhydride at 171 GPa and 4.1 K with a $^{57}$Fe Mössbauer sensor. Nuclear forward scattering tracks the local induction $B_{\rm sensor}(B_a)$, which nearly merges with the normal-state line near 1.5 T. Rather than assuming a constant-current Bean state, we solve the critical-state problem for the actual thin-disk geometry using the Shantsev--Clem--Sanchez method and compare generalized field-dependent critical-state models. Both the Kim and exponential laws reproduce the measured virgin branch, whereas the constant-$J_c$ Bean model fails. The corresponding zero-field critical-current densities, $(4.38\pm1.48)\times10^{11}$ and $(2.45\pm0.22)\times10^{11}$ A/m$^2$, respectively, are consistent in order of magnitude with transport estimates for granular YH$_6$. </details>


### [89] [Comparative First-Principles Study of the Physical Properties, Phonon-Mediated Superconductivity and Functional Potential of Technetium-Based Binary Borides TcB2, Tc2B and Tc3B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25554)
*M. Rabi Us Sani Shapon,Jubair Hossan Abir,Md. Asif Afzal,Raihana Shams Islam,Saleh Hasan Naqib*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算，比较了 TcB₂(P6/mmm, 20 GPa)、Tc₂B(I4/mcm, 0 GPa) 和 Tc₃B(P4/mmm, 0 GPa) 三种二元过渡金属硼化物的力学、电子、光学及超导性质，发现三者均为金属，其超导性由电子-声子匹配而非单纯态密度决定，Tc 分别为 24.92、11.07 和 15.12 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡金属二元硼化物的声子介导超导电性取决于金属 d 态、金属-硼键合及其耦合的晶格振动之间的平衡。作者希望厘清不同计量比和压强条件下 Tc-B 硼化物中超导性与电子结构、声子振动之间的内在关联，以及态密度（DOS）大小是否足以解释超导临界温度的差异。

Method: 采用第一性原理框架，对 P6/mmm TcB₂（20 GPa）、I4/mcm Tc₂B（0 GPa）与 P4/mmm Tc₃B（0 GPa）进行对比计算：分析机械性质（刚性、延展性趋势）、电子态密度与费米面轨道成分（Tc-4d / B-2p 混合程度）、电荷重分布、含自旋轨道耦合的金属性、光学响应（反射率与紫外吸收），并计算电子-声子耦合（EPC）以 X 求得 μ*=0.10 下的超导临界温度 Tc。

Result: 三者的机械响应差异明显：TcB₂ 在压缩条件下刚性最强，Tc₂B 延展性倾向最强，Tc₃B 介于两者之间，但三者均保持金属性。费米能级处态主要由 Tc-4d 主导，但 Tc-d/B-p 混合程度各异；电荷重分布在 TcB₂ 中更具方向性且以 B 为中心，在 Tc₂B 与 Tc₃B 中则围绕 Tc 网络更离域。含自旋轨道耦合后三者仍为金属。光学响应保持金属性且依赖电场极化方向，具有显著可见光反射率和强紫外吸收。低频率的富 Tc 振动主导电子-声子耦合。μ*=0.10 时 Tc 分别为 TcB₂ 24.92 K、Tc₂B 11.07 K、Tc₃B 15.12 K。尽管 Tc₃B 的费米面态密度 N(EF) 更高，但 TcB₂ 与 EPC 活跃的低频模式耦合最强。

Conclusion: 超导层次的排序由电子-声子匹配决定，而非仅由费米面态密度大小决定：Tc₃B 虽具有更高的 N(EF)，但 TcB₂ 因与 EPC 活跃的低频模式耦合最紧密而获得最高 Tc（24.92 K）。这表明在 Tc-B 硼化物中，理解超导性必须综合考察电子态与声子振动之间的匹配关系，而非孤立地看态密度。

Abstract: Phonon-mediated superconductivity in binary transition-metal borides reflects a balance between the metal d-states, metal-boron bonding, and the lattice vibrations that couple to them. Here, we compare P6/mmm TcB2 at 20 GPa with I4/mcm Tc2B and P4/mmm Tc3B at 0 GPa within a first-principles framework. Their mechanical responses differ appreciably: TcB2 is the most rigid under its compressed condition, Tc2B shows the strongest tendency toward ductility, and Tc3B falls between the two. Despite these differences, all three phases remain metallic. The states at the Fermi level are dominated by Tc-4d character, although the extent of Tc-d/B-p mixing varies from one compound to another. Charge redistribution is more directional and B-centered in TcB2, but more delocalized around the Tc network in Tc2B and Tc3B. Inclusion of spin-orbit coupling leaves all three compounds metallic. The optical response remains metallic and electric field polarization dependent, with substantial visible reflectivity and strong ultraviolet absorption. Low-frequency Tc rich vibrations dominate the electron-phonon coupling (EPC). With μ* = 0.10, the Tc values of TcB2, Tc2B, and Tc3B are 24.92, 11.07, and 15.12 K, respectively. Although Tc3B exhibits a higher electronic density of states at the Fermi level, N(EF), TcB2 couples most strongly to EPC-active low-frequency modes, showing that the superconducting hierarchy depends on electronic-phonon matching rather than DOS magnitude alone.

</details>
