### [90] [Dynamical Variational Theory of Stochastic Reaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25491)
*Moshir Harsh,Peter Sollich*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Plefka展开的动力学变分近似方法，用于求解具有一般化学计量比和时变速率、且处于强波动低拷贝数状态下的离散随机反应网络动力学，并引入广义自洽气泡重求和给出均值、关联与响应函数的闭式预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散随机种群动力学（如生化反应网络、基因调控、流行病传播、生态与种群遗传学）在有限种群下产生强内禀涨落，但其预测面临挑战：化学主方程无一般解析解，数值解法随系统规模扩展性差，标准近似在低拷贝数下失效。因此需要一种适用于强波动低拷贝数体系的通用解析框架。

Method: 采用反应动力学的场论表示，基于自旋玻璃物理中的Plefka展开发展动力学变分近似；对具有一般化学计量比和时变速率系统，展开得到带自洽记忆的可解非马尔可夫有效动力学；进一步引入广义自洽气泡重求和，给出均值、关联与响应函数的闭式预测，并实现带自适应时间步长的数值程序，适用于用户自定义系统。

Result: 在随机基因调控、酶动力学和感染动力学上对标验证，方法能同时准确刻画时间依赖行为和稳态，并捕捉到传统近似遗漏的由涨落驱动的效应；数值实现具备自适应时间步长。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了适用于瞬态与稳态、强涨落低拷贝数体系的随机反应动力学通用解析框架，为离散随机种群动力学网络提供了一种可广泛应用的预测工具。

Abstract: Discrete stochastic population dynamics describe systems ranging from biochemical reaction networks and gene regulation to epidemic spreading, ecology, and population genetics, where finite populations generate strong intrinsic fluctuations. Predicting their dynamics, including mean populations, fluctuations, time-lagged correlations, response functions, and steady states, remains challenging: the chemical master equation admits no general analytical solution, numerical solutions scale poorly with system size, and standard approximations fail at low copy numbers. Using a field-theoretic representation of the reaction dynamics, we develop a dynamical variational approximation for stochastic reaction networks based on the Plefka expansion from spin-glass physics. For reaction systems with general stoichiometry and time-dependent rates, the expansion yields tractable non-Markovian effective dynamics with self-consistent memory. We further introduce a generalized self-consistent bubble resummation that provides closed predictions for means, correlations, and response functions. The formulation applies broadly to discrete stochastic population dynamics on networks, and we provide a numerical implementation with adaptive time stepping for user-defined systems. We benchmark the method on stochastic gene regulation, enzyme kinetics, and infection dynamics, where it accurately captures both time-dependent behaviour and steady states, including fluctuation-driven effects missed by conventional approximations. Together, these results establish a general analytical framework for stochastic reaction dynamics across transient and stationary regimes in strongly fluctuating low-copy-number systems.

</details>


### [91] [Critical droplets and metastability in long-range chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25764)
*Laura Batini,Valerio Pagni,Nicolò Defenu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维长程相互作用伊辛链（相互作用按 1/r^{1+σ} 衰减）中一阶相变的亚稳态衰变，发现临界液滴半径按 R_c ∝ h^{-1/σ} 标度，活化势垒在 σ<1 时发散、σ=1 时对数增长、σ>1 时保持有限，并通过非局域 φ^4 场论和随机长程动力学验证了这些标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在最邻近一维伊辛链中，液滴两壁的界面能代价与尺寸无关；但在长程相互作用（1/r^{1+σ}）下，表面代价与液滴尺寸相关，并与体自由能增益竞争。作者旨在揭示长程相互作用如何改变临界液滴成核及其活化势垒对偏置场 h 的依赖规律。

Method: 作者结合理论分析与数值/解析手段：一是基于长程伊辛链的能量竞争分析，推导临界半径与势垒的标度关系；二是建立非局域 φ^4 场论，得到带代数尾巴的临界液滴解并验证半径与势垒标度；三是采用随机长程动力学独立复现临界半径的标度行为。

Result: 临界半径满足 R_c ∝ h^{-1/σ}（小 h 极限）。σ=1 是分界点：σ<1 时活化势垒随 h→0⁺ 发散；σ=1 时势垒对数增长；σ>1 时势垒保持有限，与最邻近链情形一致。非局域 φ^4 场论的临界液滴具有代数衰减尾巴，并确认了上述半径与势垒标度；随机长程动力学也独立复现了临界半径标度。

Conclusion: 长程相互作用按 1/r^{1+σ} 衰减时，一维伊辛链的成核行为由 σ 与 1 的相对大小决定：σ≤1 时表面代价随尺寸增长导致势垒发散或对数增长，σ>1 时恢复有限势垒。该结果统一了短程与长程相互作用下的亚稳态衰变图像。

Abstract: Metastable decay in first-order phase transitions proceeds through the nucleation of critical droplets. In the one-dimensional nearest-neighbor Ising chain, the cost of the two walls bounding a droplet is independent of its size. For interactions decaying as $1/r^{1+σ}$, this surface cost instead depends on the droplet size and competes with the bulk free-energy gain. The critical radius scales as $R_c\propto h^{-1/σ}$ at small $h$, where $h>0$ is the bias field. The value $σ=1$ separates two regimes: for $σ<1$, the activation barrier diverges as $h\to0^+$; at $σ=1$, it grows logarithmically; and for $σ>1$, it remains finite, as in the nearest-neighbor chain. A nonlocal $φ^4$ field theory yields critical droplets with algebraic tails and confirms the predicted radius and barrier scalings, while stochastic long-range dynamics independently reproduces the critical-radius scaling.

</details>


### [92] [Chasing the KPZ exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26166)
*Luciano C. Lapas,Márcio S. Gomes-Filho,Fernando A. Oliveira*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将KPZ方程非线性项视为作用于基底层分形子集上的有效噪声的方法，从而在d维中导出粗糙度指数的上下界1/(d+1)≤α≤1/d，并审视其假设、与四十年理论与实验对照，指出需在三维及以上开展检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KPZ方程问世四十年，在一维已精确求解并被实验证实，二维激子极化激元凝聚中也观测到其标度行为；但一维以上没有一个指数被精确知道，甚至上临界维度的存在性都存在争议。作者希望表明，无需等待精确解也能给出关于指数范围的可靠论断。

Method: 将KPZ非线性项视为一种有效噪声，该有效噪声作用于基底层的一个分形子集上；基于这一图像推导出d维空间中粗糙度指数α的上下界1/(d+1)≤α≤1/d。作者进一步检验该论证背后的假设，并对照四十年来理论、数值模拟及最新实验结果进行交叉验证。

Result: 得到d维KPZ粗糙度指数满足1/(d+1)≤α≤1/d的界。文章系统梳理了这些界所依赖的假设，并将其与理论、模拟及新实验（尤其是二维极化子凝聚体实验）相对照，指出最关键的检验应集中在三维及更高维度，以确立或推翻这些界。

Conclusion: 无需等待精确解也能对高维KPZ指数形成可靠约束；所提出的1/(d+1)≤α≤1/d界为理解和检验高维KPZ标度行为提供了框架，其成立与否主要取决于三维及以上维度的进一步理论与实验检验。

Abstract: Forty years after it was written down, the Kardar--Parisi--Zhang (KPZ) equation is the paradigm of scale invariance far from equilibrium. In one dimension it has been solved exactly and confirmed by precision experiments, and its scaling has now been observed in two-dimensional polariton condensates. Above one dimension, however, not a single exponent is known exactly, and even the existence of an upper critical dimension is disputed. In this Perspective we argue that firm statements need not wait for exact solutions. Treating the nonlinearity as an effective noise that acts on a fractal subset of the substrate leads to bounds on the roughness exponent, $1/(d+1)\leα\le 1/d$ in $d$ dimensions. We examine the assumptions behind these bounds, confront them with four decades of theory, simulations and the new experiments, and identify the tests---foremost in three and more dimensions---that would establish or refute them.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱统计常被用作区分可积模型与混沌模型的探针，但本文发现一个反例：一个标准Yang-Baxter框架下的可积、平移不变且干净的模型，却表现出Wigner-Dyson谱统计，挑战了"可积模型必然对应泊松谱统计"这一传统认知，促使作者探究其背后的真实机制。

Method: 作者构造并分析了一个具有开放边界条件的可积自旋链，发现其具有均匀指数简并；切换到周期边界条件后，简并被劈裂。进一步结合全局对称性对Hilbert空间进行分扇区，检查各扇区的谱统计（Wigner-Dyson vs 泊松），并提出非局域Hilbert空间碎片化作为解释谱性质的关键机制，比较了开放与周期边界条件下碎片化程度的差异。

Result: 在开放边界条件下模型呈现均匀的指数简并；在周期边界条件下该简并被劈裂，且少量由全局对称性选出的扇区显示出Wigner-Dyson谱统计。该模型干净、平移不变且符合标准Yang-Baxter框架。谱异常的根源被归因于非局域的Hilbert空间碎片化，其程度在开放与周期边界条件下不同。

Conclusion: 该模型表明Wigner-Dyson谱统计并不必然意味着量子混沌，单纯以谱统计来区分可积与混沌会失效。非局域Hilbert空间碎片化是产生异常谱关联的关键，且模型的动力学本质上仍非混沌。

Abstract: In quantum many-body physics spectral statistics is often used as a probe to distinguish integrable and chaotic models. We find a special case of an integrable spin chain with unusual spectral properties. With open boundary conditions the model has homogeneous exponential degeneracies; these are split for periodic boundary conditions. In the latter case, we find that a small number of sectors (selected by a global symmetry) showcase Wigner-Dyson spectral statistics, which usually signals quantum chaos. Our model is clean, translationally invariant, and it fits into the standard Yang-Baxter framework. We identify Hilbert space fragmentation as the key mechanism behind the spectral properties. The fragmentation is non-local, and its extent is different in the case of open and periodic boundary conditions. We argue that the dynamics of the model is not chaotic despite the unusual spectral correlations.

</details>

TL;DR: 该论文发展了一个鞅（martingale）框架，为一般马尔可夫过程的可加观测量给出精确的有限时间涨落-响应恒等式，并将方差分解为初始系综贡献与噪声贡献，进而推导出不等式、变分原理及频域界，应用于欠阻尼动力学时得到可任意有限时间饱和的摩擦-响应热力学不确定关系（TUR），并证明了速度分辨电流对传统TUR的指数级强违反及惯性振子的耗散-相干界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间积分观测量的涨落与响应均可实验测量，二者之间的关系能够揭示维持非平衡态所需的耗散。已有研究中缺乏一个统一的、精确的有限时间框架来同时描述一般马尔可夫过程（含扩散与跳跃过程）的涨落与响应，尤其是欠阻尼（惯性）动力学下的情形，因此亟需建立这样的框架以给出精确恒等式及其推论。

Method: 作者以Doob鞅为核心工具，为可加观测量构造鞅表示：将观测量方差精确分解为“继承自初始系综的部分”与“观测期间由噪声产生的部分”；在固定制备条件下，观测量均值对任意容许扰动的响应等于Doob鞅与表征扰动的第二个鞅之间的协方差。对扩散和跳跃过程，单一鞅振幅同时决定涨落与响应。由这些恒等式进一步导出不等式、变分原理以及多变量和频域界的紧界，其“代价”由对应物理扰动设定。框架被应用于欠阻尼动力学、过阻尼动力学及跳跃过程。

Result: 1) 得到观测量方差的精确分解恒等式；2) 在欠阻尼动力学下获得可任意有限时间饱和的摩擦-响应TUR，并构成熵产生的变分原理；3) 证明速度分辨电流即使在线性高斯动力学中也会对传统TUR产生指数级强的违反；4) 推导出极限环的弱噪声耗散-相干界，表明惯性振子仅在频率对摩擦不敏感时才能打破过阻尼界；5) 对过阻尼动力学与跳跃过程，该框架恢复并推广了近期涨落-响应恒等式，并识别了相应不确定关系达到饱和的条件。

Conclusion: 该鞅框架为马尔可夫过程的有限时间涨落-响应关系提供了统一而精确的刻画，将方差来源、响应机制与物理扰动代价联系起来；其推论（摩擦-响应TUR及其变分原理、速度分辨电流对传统TUR的强违反、惯性极限环的耗散-相干界）显著拓展了热力学不确定关系在欠阻尼与惯性系统中的应用范围，并厘清了相关界的饱和条件。

Abstract: Fluctuations and responses of time-integrated observables are both experimentally measurable, and relations between them can reveal the dissipation that sustains a nonequilibrium state. Here we develop a martingale framework that gives exact finite-time fluctuation-response identities for additive observables of general Markov processes. We obtain an exact identity for the variance of an observable in terms of its Doob martingale, which splits the total variance into a part inherited from the initial ensemble and a part generated by noise during the observation. We show that, at fixed preparation, the response of the mean of the observable to any admissible perturbation is the covariance between the Doob martingale and a second martingale that represents the perturbation. For diffusions and jump processes, a single martingale amplitude determines both the fluctuations and the response of the observable. These identities yield inequalities, variational principles, and multivariate and frequency-domain bounds, whose costs are set by the corresponding physical perturbations. Applying the framework to underdamped dynamics, we obtain a friction-response thermodynamic uncertainty relation (TUR) that can be saturated at any finite time and thus gives a variational principle for the entropy production. We also demonstrate exponentially strong violations of the conventional TUR by velocity-resolved currents, even in linear Gaussian dynamics, and derive a weak-noise dissipation-coherence bound for limit cycles, which shows that inertial oscillators can beat the overdamped bound only if their frequency is weakly sensitive to friction. For overdamped dynamics and jump processes, the framework recovers and extends recent fluctuation-response identities and identifies when the corresponding uncertainty relations are saturated.

</details>


### [95] [First-passage and correlated reset-count statistics under threshold and Poissonian resetting in a two-dimensional wedge geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26345)
*Fazil Najeeb,Arnab Pal,V. V Prasad*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维开放楔形区域中布朗目标搜索问题里阈值重置（TR）与泊松重置的相互作用。作者先用 Biswas 等人的更新框架求得仅有 TR 时首次通过时间（FPT）的精确一二阶矩，其密度呈代数长尾 $t^{-1-\pi/2\alpha}$；随后构建基于随机变量的通用更新形式推导两种重置机制组合下的平均 FPT。结果表明，当 $0<\alpha<\pi/4$ 时，底层 TR 动力学的变异系数决定泊松重置是加速还是阻碍搜索；当 $\alpha\ge\pi/4$ 时泊松重置总能降低 MFPT。文章还推导了两种重置机制的联合计数统计，并通过 Pearson 相关系数刻画其相互依赖，发现随着泊松重置率变化出现弱相关到强相关的交叉，且该交叉行为对初始位置有非平凡依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多种（竞争性）重置机制共同作用于随机搜索过程时如何影响搜索效率，特别是阈值重置与泊松重置的相互耦合，此前缺乏统一的理论框架来处理这种组合效应及两种机制之间的统计关联。

Method: 1) 针对仅有阈值重置（TR）的情形，利用 Biswas 等人在 Phys. Rev. Lett. 135, 227101 (2025) 提出的更新框架，求得 FPT 的精确一、二阶矩，并分析其密度的代数长时尾部 $t^{-1-\pi/2\alpha}$。2) 为组合 TR 与泊松重置，构造一个基于随机变量的通用更新形式，推导组合机制下的平均 FPT。3) 通过变异系数判定泊松重置对搜索的加速/阻碍效应，并利用 Pearson 相关系数刻画两种重置机制的联合计数统计及其相互依赖性。

Result: 1) 仅 TR 时，FPT 密度呈代数衰减的长尾 $t^{-1-\pi/2\alpha}$，并得到精确的一、二阶矩。2) 组合机制下：当 $0<\alpha<\pi/4$ 时，泊松重置是否加速搜索取决于底层 TR 动力学的变异系数；当 $\alpha\ge\pi/4$ 时，泊松重置总是降低 MFPT。3) 联合计数统计表明两重置机制的相关性随泊松重置率变化，出现从弱相关到强相关的交叉，且交叉行为对搜索初始位置存在非平凡依赖。

Conclusion: 文章给出了一个可推广的通用随机变量更新框架，用于研究竞争性重置机制的相互耦合，揭示了楔角与泊松重置率共同决定搜索效率加速/阻碍的相图，以及两机制间相关性的交叉现象，为更一般几何下竞争重置机制的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study the interplay between threshold resetting (TR) and Poissonian resetting in a Brownian target search problem confined to a two-dimensional open wedge of apex angle $α$. The boundary at zero angle acts as an absorbing target, while encounters with the boundary at $α$ trigger TR, instantaneously restarting the search from its initial position. For TR alone, using the renewal framework developed by Biswas et al. in Phys. Rev. Lett. 135, 227101 (2025), we obtain exact first and second moments of the first-passage time (FPT), whose density exhibits an algebraic long-time tail $t^{-1-\fracπ{2α}}$. To combine the two resetting mechanisms, we thereafter develop a generic random-variable-based renewal formalism to derive the combined mean FPT. We show that the coefficient of variation of the underlying TR dynamics determines whether Poissonian resetting accelerates or hinders the search for $0<α<π/4$, whereas for $α\geqπ/4$, Poissonian resetting always reduces the MFPT. Furthermore, we derive the joint count statistics of the two resetting mechanisms and characterize their mutual dependence through Pearson's correlation coefficient. Interestingly, we uncover a crossover from weak to strong correlations as the Poissonian resetting rate is varied, with the crossover behavior exhibiting a nontrivial dependence on the initial search position. Our framework provides a basis for studying competing resetting mechanisms in more general geometries.

</details>

Result: 所得闭合形式无需相互作用势，仅需单一态点的虚时密度关联及其理想气体参考即可计算；该方法能恢复经典结果，并估计出经典关系遗漏的熵。作者在弱相互作用玻色子、环上相互作用费米子以及温稠密电子气上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将经典过量熵估计推广至量子系统，弥补了经典关系因忽略动能改变、离域与交换效应而遗漏的熵，并可进一步扩展其他经典熵估计器至量子体系。

Abstract: In the classical limit, maximum-entropy inference yields an estimate of the excess entropy from the static structure factor through a single momentum sum. Quantum mechanically this estimate fails, because interactions also change the kinetic energy, while delocalization and exchange add information that equal-time correlations do not contain. We show that imaginary-time density correlations contain this information, and that with them the classical relation carries over to quantum systems. Such correlations are available from path-integral simulations and X-ray Thomson scattering. Relative-entropy inference over imaginary-time paths, with an ideal Bose or Fermi gas as reference, gives closed forms for the excess entropy and free energies, within the same Gaussian approximation as the classical relation. They recover the classical result and estimate entropy that the classical relation misses. We test them on weakly interacting bosons, interacting fermions on a ring, and the warm dense electron gas. The closed forms need only imaginary-time density correlations and their ideal-gas reference at a single state point, without using the interaction potential. Our approach can extend other classical entropy estimators to quantum systems.

</details>

Abstract: As anything not in equilibrium is, by definition, nonequilibrium, there is no universally agreed-upon set of topics that can constitute an exhaustive presentation of nonequilibrium statistical mechanics. At the same time, nonequilibrium statistical mechanics has transcended traditional physics to become a powerful language for complex systems. Prompted by an enthusiastic curiosity about complex systems, these cursory lecture notes attempt to elucidate some introductory concepts and techniques used in classical nonequilibrium statistical mechanics, which find successful applications not only in foundational physics, but also across other fields such as biology, ecology, economics, and sociology. Except for the specific synthesis and presentation of the material, which assumes basic familiarity with equilibrium statistical mechanics, the author claims no originality.

</details>


### [98] [Exact fermionic decay length and Majorana localization in the sign-alternating kagomé Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26529)
*Shahane Khachatryan,Hrachya Babujian,Ara SEdrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文推导了在横向动量 p=π/2 处符号交替 kagomé Ising 模型的精确费米子衰减长度，其倒数由三个耦合参数的解析表达式给出，并揭示了其发散点对应两条经典临界分支，以及在标度极限下与横场 Ising 链能隙闭合条件的联系，同时证明强键 Yang–Baxter 族无法在固定基拟设下连续延拓到有限耦合内部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究符号交替（sign-alternating）kagomé Ising 模型在特定横向动量下的精确费米子衰减行为，以理解其临界性与拓扑性质，并厘清模型耦合参数空间中解析延拓的可行性。

Method: 在固定横向动量 p=π/2 下，利用三个无量纲耦合 x=tanh K1、y=tanh K2、z=tanh K3，通过解析推导得到每格衰减长度的倒数 ξ_f^{-1}=|ln(U/|V|)|，其中 U=(1-x²)(1+y²z²)、V=(1+x²)(y²-z²)；进一步在同时强键与弱键标度下与行转移矩阵得到的横场 Ising 链在动量 0 和 π 处的能隙闭合条件对应，并将经典衰减长度与 Jordan–Wigner 费米子链 Majorana 端模式的局域化长度相联系；同时考察固定基拟设下带一个消失键方向的强键 Yang–Baxter 族的连续延拓。

Result: 衰减长度在 U=|V| 处发散，V 的符号区分两条经典临界分支；这两条分支在同时强-弱键标度下分别对应横场 Ising 链在动量 0 和 π 的能隙闭合条件；经典衰减长度趋于 Jordan–Wigner 费米子链 Majorana 端模式的局域化长度；并证明正则强键 Yang–Baxter 族无法在含一个消失键方向的固定基拟设下连续延拓到有限耦合内部。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了符号交替 kagomé Ising 模型费米子衰减长度的精确解析结果，建立了临界发散、能隙闭合与拓扑端模式局域化之间的对应关系，并否定了特定拟设下强键 Yang–Baxter 族向有限耦合内部的连续延拓可能性。

Abstract: We derive an exact fermionic decay length for the sign-alternating kagomé Ising model at fixed transverse momentum $p=π/2$. Writing $x=\tanh K_1$, $y=\tanh K_2$, and $z=\tanh K_3$ for the three dimensionless couplings, the inverse length per lattice cell is $ξ_{\rm f}^{-1}=|\ln(U/|V|)|$, where $U=(1-x^2)(1+y^2z^2)$ and $V=(1+x^2)(y^2-z^2)$. The length diverges at $U=|V|$, with the sign of $V$ selecting one of two classical critical branches. In a simultaneous strong- and weak-bond scaling, these branches become the gap-closing conditions at momenta $0$ and $π$ of the transverse-field Ising chain obtained from the row transfer matrix. On the topological side, the classical decay length tends to the localization length of a Majorana end mode of the Jordan--Wigner fermion chain. We also prove that the regular strong-bond Yang--Baxter family cannot be continued to the finite-coupling interior within the fixed-basis ansatz with one vanishing bond direction.

</details>


### [99] [Field theory and the biquadratic spin-1 chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26610)
*Luisa Eck,Paul Fendley*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 反铁磁双二次自旋-1链具有能隙、SU(3)对称性和可积性，其低能物理通常被认为由θ=π的CP²非线性σ模型描述。本文用Bethe ansatz证明其低能激发是解禁闭的畴壁，并提出一个非洛伦兹不变的可积场论来解释该模型可积而场论不可积的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一个谜团：格点模型（反铁磁双二次自旋-1链）是可积的，而其低能有效场论（CP²非线性σ模型）通常被认为不可积。作者试图找到一个可积的场论，使其具有相同的低能极限，从而调和这一矛盾。

Method: 利用Bethe ansatz分析低能激发，将其解释为解禁闭的畴壁；通过添加更高的守恒荷并将链调节到临界点，获得保持可积性的标度极限。该极限中能隙以二次方式闭合，因此应由非相对论场论描述，而非相对论σ模型。作者构造了该场论并描述其两粒子散射矩阵。

Result: 低能激发可解释为解禁闭的畴壁；找到了一个非洛伦兹不变的可积场论，它与原模型具有相同的低能极限；能隙二次闭合表明极限对应非相对论场论；给出了该场论的两粒子散射矩阵。

Conclusion: 原格点模型的可积性与低能σ模型不可积之间的矛盾，可通过一个非洛伦兹不变的可积场论解决；保持可积性的标度极限需要通过添加更高守恒荷并调节至临界点获得，且该极限本质上是非相对论的。

Abstract: The antiferromagnetic biquadratic spin-1 chain is gapped, $SU(3)$ symmetric, and integrable. Standard arguments suggest that its low-energy physics is described by the $\mathbb{CP}^2$ nonlinear sigma model at $θ=π$. Using the Bethe ansatz, we show that its low-energy excitations can be interpreted as deconfined domain walls, just as in the sigma model. However, the lattice model is integrable, whereas the field theory is generally believed not to be. We propose a solution to this mystery by finding an integrable field theory with the same low-energy limit but which is not Lorentz invariant. The integrability-preserving scaling limit is obtained by adding a higher conserved charge and tuning the chain to criticality. The gap closes quadratically, so that the limit must be described by some nonrelativistic field theory rather than the relativistic sigma model. We describe the two-particle scattering matrix of this field theory.

</details>

### [100] [Canonical and Canonoid Transformations of Hamiltonian Systems on $q$-Cosymplectic and $q$-Contact Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24961)
*Giuseppe Marmo,Cristina Sardón,Xuefeng Zhao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 q-余辛流形与 q-接触流形上的典范变换与 quasi-canonoid（canonoid）变换，建立局部正规形、刻画无穷小自同构与 Noether 型关系，并用相对论质量壳约束和磁单极场中的电荷等例子加以说明，最后构造非均匀 2-接触结构并证明其拓扑障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为预辛（presymplectic）与预接触（precontact）几何引入 q-余辛与 q-接触结构（附加一个 1-形式和一族归一化的 Reeb 向量场），以研究源自约束力学系统的典范与 canonoid 变换。现有均匀理论的局限（如非均匀结构无法纳入）促使推广到非均匀情形。

Method: 在 q-余辛与均匀 q-接触情形下，建立局部正规形；刻画无穷小自同构；得到对称性与守恒量/耗散量之间的 Noether 型关系。对以 X 为生成元的一参数 canonoid 变换群，证明李括号 [X, X_H] 为哈密顿量并求出其哈密顿函数。在均匀 q-接触情形，定义形式的同时常数缩放为每个哈密顿量提供 canonoid 变换。用正则相对论质量壳约束和磁单极场中电荷的整体拉格朗日描述做几何构造示例。对两个磁单极构造非均匀 2-接触结构，证明无法存在以指定规范生成元为 Reeb 场的均匀结构；并讨论无理性相关磁荷对中间商空间（单一接触流形）的障碍。

Result: 得到 q-余辛与均匀 q-接触情形的局部正规形、无穷小自同构刻画及 Noether 型关系；证明 [X, X_H] 为哈密顿量并确定其哈密顿函数；在均匀 q-接触中定义形式的同时常数缩放给出对每个哈密顿量都成立的 canonoid 变换。示例恢复自由相对论粒子的总角动量和电荷-磁单极系统的庞加莱角动量，并区分约束轨道参数与物理时间、澄清规范方向的作用。对两个磁单极构造了非均匀 2-接触结构，证明存在拓扑障碍使其不能容纳为以指定规范生成元为 Reeb 场的均匀结构；无理性相关的磁荷进一步阻碍产生单一接触流形的中间商。

Conclusion: 均匀 q-接触理论在描述如双磁单极等系统时存在局限，需要推广到非均匀结构；本文示例明确区分了约束轨道参数与物理时间，澄清了规范方向，并恢复了相对论与磁单极系统的角动量等守恒量。

Abstract: $q$-Cosymplectic and $q$-contact structures equip presymplectic and precontact geometry with additional one-forms and a normalized family of Reeb vector fields. We study canonical and canonoid transformations relative to these data, with particular attention to their origin in constrained mechanical systems. In the $q$-cosymplectic and uniform $q$-contact cases, we establish local normal forms, characterize infinitesimal automorphisms, and obtain Noether-type relations between symmetries and conserved or dissipated quantities. For a one-parameter group of canonoid transformations with generator $X$, we show that $[X,X_H]$ is Hamiltonian and determine its Hamiltonian function. In the uniform $q$-contact case, simultaneous constant rescalings of the defining forms provide canonoid transformations for every Hamiltonian. The geometric constructions are illustrated by regular relativistic mass-shell constraints and by global Lagrangian descriptions of a charge in magnetic monopole fields. These examples distinguish parameters along constraint orbits from physical time and clarify the role of gauge directions. They also recover the total angular momentum of free relativistic particles and the Poincaré angular momentum of the charge--monopole system. For two monopoles, we construct a non-uniform $2$-contact structure and prove a topological obstruction to a uniform structure with the prescribed gauge generators as Reeb fields. Irrationally related magnetic charges additionally obstruct the intermediate quotient that would produce a single contact manifold. This example identifies a limitation of the uniform theory and motivates its extension to non-uniform structures.

</details>


### [101] [On Hölder Equicontinuity of the Integrated Density of States for Random Schrödinger Operators on $\mathbb{Z}^d$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25077)
*Jan Fischer,David Hasler*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究$\mathbb{Z}^d$上随机薛定谔算子$H_\lambda = H_0 + \lambda V$的积分态密度，在跳跃矩阵满足正则性假设下，证明其Hölder连续性，指数最高可达$2/3$，且与无序强度$\lambda$无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机薛定谔算子的积分态密度(IDS)是表征能谱分布的核心量。其正则性（特别是Hölder连续性）是研究Anderson局域化、能谱边界附近态密度行为的重要工具。以往结果中Hölder指数常依赖于无序强度或对跳跃矩阵有更强限制，本文旨在在较弱的正则性假设下，给出与无序强度无关的统一Hölder连续性估计。

Method: 考虑$\mathbb{Z}^d$上的随机算子$H_\lambda = H_0 + \lambda V$，其中$H_0$为平移不变的跳跃（hopping）矩阵，$V$为单位点势能独立同分布且具有绝对连续分布。对$H_0$施加正则性假设，借助随机势的绝对连续性，通过Wegner型估计与分数阶矩等技巧，建立积分态密度的定量连续性估计。

Result: 在跳跃矩阵的正则性假设下，证明积分态密度$N_\lambda(E)$关于能量$E$是Hölder连续的，Hölder指数可达到$2/3$；该指数对任意无序强度$\lambda$一致成立，即与$\lambda$无关。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，仅需跳跃矩阵满足适当的正则性条件，即可获得积分态密度指数为$2/3$的Hölder连续性，且对无序强度具有一致性，推广并改进了此前的相关结论。

Abstract: We study a random Schrödinger operator $H_λ= H_0 + λV$ on $\mathbb{Z}^d$ consisting of a translation invariant hopping matrix $H_0$ and a random potential $V$, given by independently distributed single site potentials with absolutely continuous distributions. Assuming a regularity assumption for the hopping matrix $H_0$, we show Hölder continuity of the integrated density of states of $H_λ$ up to exponents $2/3$, independently of the disorder strength $λ$.

</details>


### [102] [Interacting framework for the free magnetic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25285)
*Giuseppe De Nittis*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文是连续费米子在均匀磁场中的严格多体理论系列的第二篇，基于磁相干态框架在CAR代数上诱导的渐近拟局部结构，研究自由动力学。作者证明Bogoliubov动力学保持该局部结构当且仅当它由Landau哈密顿量的函数生成；对此类动力学给出跳跃系数的闭式表达，证明Lieb-Robinson速度为零，并确定几乎局部代数何时落在生成元的定义域内；Landau哈密顿量对应的动力学是周期的。进一步刻画了所有温度下的基态与KMS态，证明其在磁平移下的遍历性，并将正温KMS态恢复为局部Gibbs态的热力学极限。最后把精确跳跃算子与Hofstadter模型联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是建立有均匀磁场时连续费米子的严格多体理论。关键出发点是由磁相干态框架在CAR代数上诱导的渐近拟局部结构。本文旨在验证该局部结构对自由（非相互作用）动力学是否自洽，为后续研究微扰与相互作用磁动力学奠定基础。

Method: 在磁相干态框架诱导的渐近拟局部结构下分析自由Bogoliubov动力学。首先刻画哪些Bogoliubov动力学保持该局部结构（等价于由Landau哈密顿量的函数生成）；对此类动力学闭式算出跳跃系数；证明Lieb-Robinson界中速度为零；判定几乎局部代数落入生成元定义域的条件。然后研究Landau哈密顿量动力学的周期性，并利用磁平移与热力学极限方法刻画基态和KMS态，最后建立跳跃算子与Hofstadter模型的对应。

Result: ①Bogoliubov动力学保持渐近拟局部结构当且仅当由Landau哈密顿量的函数生成；②对此类动力学跳跃系数有闭式表达；③Lieb-Robinson速度为零，表明信息传播速度为0；④Landau哈密顿量动力学是周期的；⑤给出任意温度下基态与KMS态的完整描述，证明其在磁平移下遍历；⑥正温KMS态可恢复为局部Gibbs态的热力学极限；⑦精确跳跃算子与Hofstadter模型相关联。

Conclusion: 磁相干态框架诱导的局部结构对自由磁性动力学是自洽且一致的数学框架，可作为后续微扰和相互作用磁性多体动力学研究的可靠起点。

Abstract: This is the second paper of a program aimed at a rigorous many--body theory of continuum fermions in a uniform magnetic field, built on the asymptotic quasi--local structure that the magnetic coherent--state frame induces on the CAR algebra. Here we study the free dynamics. We show that a Bogoliubov dynamics preserves the local structure exactly if and only if it is generated by a function of the Landau Hamiltonian. For this class we compute the hopping coefficients in closed form, prove that the Lieb--Robinson velocity vanishes, and determine when the almost--local algebra lies in the domain of the generator. For the Landau Hamiltonian the dynamics turns out to be periodic. We then describe the ground states and the KMS states at every temperature, prove their ergodicity under magnetic translations, and recover the KMS states at positive temperature as thermodynamic limits of local Gibbs states. Finally, we relate the exact hopping operator to the Hofstadter model. These results confirm that the frame--induced local structure is a consistent setting for the perturbed and interacting magnetic dynamics to be studied next.

</details>


### [103] [Painlevé transcendents in the defocusing Hirota equation with non-zero boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25308)
*Shuning Lin,Engui Fan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用反散射变换所对应的 Riemann–Hilbert 问题，通过 $\bar{\partial}$-最速下降法与双重尺度极限技巧，研究具有非零边界条件的散焦 Hirota 方程 Cauchy 问题在两类过渡区域中的长时间渐近，并将其表示为 Painlevé II 方程的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hirota 方程在非零边界条件下可通过反散射变换刻画，但其解的长时间渐近行为在研究边缘区域（即相速度临界点附近）尚未完全清楚。作者旨在完整刻画解在两处临界相速度 $a=-4$ 与 $a=-8$ 附近的过渡区域中的渐近展开，以补充全局长时间渐近理论。

Method: 作者将散焦 Hirota 方程的 Cauchy 问题表述为一个 Riemann–Hilbert 问题，进而结合 $\bar{\partial}$-最速下降法与双重尺度极限技巧，对两个缩放过渡区域 $\mathcal{P}_a=\{(x,t):|x/t-a|t^{2/3}<C\}$（$a\in\{-4,-8\}$）进行细致的渐近分析。

Result: 在通用情形下，两个过渡区域内的长时间渐近展开可用 Hastings–McLeod 型 Painlevé II 解表示；在非通用情形下，则用 Ablowitz–Segur 型解表示。

Conclusion: 该工作完整给出了散焦 Hirota 方程非零边界条件 Cauchy 问题在临界边缘过渡区域中的长时间渐近行为，揭示了其与 Painlevé II 超越函数（Hastings–McLeod 与 Ablowitz–Segur 两类解）之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We consider the Cauchy problem for the defocusing Hirota equation with non-zero boundary conditions \begin{align*} &iq_t+ q_{xx}-2\left(|q|^2-1\right)q +i\left(q_{xxx}-6|q|^2q_x\right)=0,\\ &q(x,0)=q_0(x)\to\pm 1, \quad x\to\pm\infty, \end{align*} which can be characterized using a Riemann-Hilbert problem through the inverse scattering transform. With the $\bar{\partial}$-steepest descent method and double scaling limit technique, we derive the long-time asymptotics of the solution in two transition regions \begin{align*}
  \mathcal{P}_{a}
  :=\left\{(x,t)\in\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}:
  \left| {x}/{t}-a\right|t^{2/3}<C\right\},
  \quad a\in\{-4,-8\}, \end{align*} where $C>0$ is a constant. It is shown that the asymptotic expansions in two transition regions can be expressed in terms of the Hastings--McLeod solution of the Painlevé II equation in the generic case and Ablowitz--Segur solutions in the non-generic case.

</details>


### [104] [Semiclassical Averaging and Temporal Fluctuations of Quantum Dark Solitons in the Lieb-Liniger Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25343)
*Kota Kaneko,Kohei Motegi,Jun Sato*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究Lieb–Liniger模型中量子暗孤子态的时间演化，提出在弱耦合大系统极限下其密度演化可由Gross–Pitaevskii单孤子密度的正则动量平均描述，并数值验证该对应关系，进而分析密度缺口扩散、渐近衰变及时间涨落统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子暗孤子态（单空穴yrast态的等权叠加）的时间演化与Gross–Pitaevskii经典孤子之间的对应关系尚不明确：量子叠加导致密度缺口随时间扩散，而经典孤子形状保持，需要建立二者在特定极限下的定量联系。

Method: 考虑同时取弱耦合（排斥耦合常数c→0）和大系统（粒子数N与环周长L→∞）极限，保持平均密度n̄=N/L与标度环周长Λ=L√(c n̄)固定，标度时间τ=c n̄ t。提出标准化量子密度及其演化由归一化周期单孤子GP密度的正则动量平均给出，显式按正则动量参数化GP孤子族。在无限线极限下推导初始平均剖面的解析表达式，证明其密度缺口按距离的五次方反比衰变，并将其相对背景的偏差傅里叶变换表示为Bickley–Naylor函数Ki₃的级数。进一步研究长时间模方差与GP傅里叶系数二阶矩的关系，并用yrast跃迁频率区分初始退相时间尺度与有限时间涨落平均的更长尺度。

Result: 数值证据支持量子密度演化由正则动量平均的GP单孤子密度描述的猜想；不同孤子速度解释了平均密度缺口的扩散；无限线极限下得到初始剖面的解析表达式及反五次幂衰变律；数值比较支持长期模方差与GP傅里叶系数二阶矩的关系；yrast跃迁频率成功区分了初始退相时间尺度与更长涨落平均尺度。

Conclusion: 在弱耦合大系统极限下，量子暗孤子态的密度演化可理解为GP单孤子解的正则动量平均，从而用经典孤子速度差异解释量子缺口的扩散现象；该框架给出初始剖面的精确解析刻画与涨落的统计描述，为量子暗孤子与GP孤子间的对应提供了定量理论。

Abstract: We study the time evolution of quantum dark-soliton states of the Lieb--Liniger model, introduced by Sato--Kanamoto--Kaminishi--Deguchi as equal-weight superpositions of one-hole yrast states, and their relation to Gross--Pitaevskii (GP) solitons. We consider a simultaneous weak-coupling and large-system limit in which the repulsive coupling constant $c$ tends to zero and the particle number $N$ and ring circumference $L$ tend to infinity, with the mean density $\bar n=N/L$ and scaled circumference $Λ=L\sqrt{c\bar n}$ fixed. We conjecture that, in this limit, the normalized quantum density and its evolution at fixed scaled time $τ=c\bar n t$ are described by a canonical-momentum average of normalized periodic one-soliton GP densities, each evolving at its own velocity. We explicitly parametrize the GP family by canonical momentum and provide numerical evidence for this correspondence. The different velocities explain the spreading of the averaged density notch despite shape preservation by individual solitons. In the infinite-line limit, we derive an analytic expression for the initial averaged profile, establish an inverse-fifth-power decay of its density deficit with distance from the soliton center, and express the Fourier transform of its deviation from the background as a series of Bickley--Naylor functions $\operatorname{Ki}_3$. Following the spreading of the initial notch, we investigate temporal density fluctuations of the quantum dark solitons and propose a relation between long-time mode variances and second moments of GP Fourier coefficients, supported by numerical comparisons of individual mode variances and the total variance. Yrast transition frequencies distinguish the initial dephasing time scale from the longer scale associated with finite-time averaging of fluctuations.

</details>


### [105] [A Faà di Bruno Convolution Identity in Parafermion Operator Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25455)
*Sam Kault*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出 Faà di Bruno 卷积恒等式的详细组合证明，该恒等式用于仲费米子算符乘积计算，证明了更奇异项的抵消及主导贡献对多项式指标的加法合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仲费米子算符乘积计算中需要用到 Faà di Bruno 卷积恒等式，以说明潜在的更奇异项相互抵消，并确认主导贡献按多项式指标可加地组合；此前文献仅陈述该恒等式而缺乏一般性推导。

Method: 采用详细的组合证明方法，对 Faà di Bruno 卷积恒等式给出一般性推导；该证明源自作者博士论文。

Result: 恒等式成立，确立了潜在更奇异项的抵消，并表明主导贡献将多项式指标可加地组合。

Conclusion: 该组合证明为早期仲费米子文献中仅陈述的恒等式提供了一般性推导，完善了相关算符乘积计算的理论基础。

Abstract: We give a detailed combinatorial proof of a Faà di Bruno convolution identity used in parafermion operator-product calculations. The identity establishes the cancellation of potentially more singular terms and shows that the leading contribution combines the polynomial indices additively. The proof, originating in the author's doctoral thesis, provides a general derivation of an identity stated in earlier parafermion literature.

</details>


### [106] [Block decimation and perfect actions for $p$-adic valued path integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25770)
*Su Hu,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 p 进（$\mathbb{C}_p$ 值）量子力学路径积分中的有限划分传播子及其变分/运动学重分块（decimation）流。核心发现是：在 p 进框架下，时间步 $p^n$ 实范数小恰对应 p 进范数大，因此精化与粗化互换角色，各自给出一个极限。精化方向下，支撑在 $\mathbb{Z}_p$ 上、以收敛幂级数表示且不带时间步权重的势能，经有限步后收敛（误差 $p^{-M}$）到尺度协变（perfect）作用量，由 Volkenborn 积分控制；带权重时极限不存在。粗化方向下，谐振子的变分流可闭式求解：格点角度逐次乘 $p$，耦合满足 Chebyshev 递推，无焦散，重标度作用量收敛到重整化质量 $m_0\sin_p(\theta_0)/\theta_0$ 的自由作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前引入了针对 $\mathbb{C}_p$ 值波函数的有限划分传播子，其中间位置对原子测度积分，迭代积分在端点间等距链上求值作用量，无需对涨落积分。本文旨在理解这一"运动学重分块"（经典完美作用量背后变分重分块在自由作用量处的 Fréchet 导数）在 p 进实数与实数范数关系下的极限行为，即精化（紫外）与粗化（红外）方向互换后各产生何种极限，并刻画其共性（尺度协变完美作用量）与解析可解性。

Method: 利用 p 进分析，把传播子构造为对有限划分的迭代积分，并视每个划分层次为两点格点作用量的精确树级块重分块（decimation），不涉及涨落积分。这一重分块定义为变分重分块在自由作用量处的 Fréchet 导数。借助时间步 $p^n$ 在实范数小与 p 进范数大的对偶关系，分别研究精化方向（势能支撑在 $\mathbb{Z}_p$、收敛幂级数、无时间步权重）与粗化方向（固定频率的谐振子）。精化方向用 Volkenborn 积分刻画对角线附近的极限；粗化方向对变分流封闭求解，得到格点角度乘 $p$ 的迭代、Chebyshev 型耦合递推及重标度极限；并通过线性化映射在多项式作用量上的对角化计算本征值 $p^{2-b}$。

Result: 1）精化方向：支撑于 $\mathbb{Z}_p$、由收敛幂级数给出且不带时间步权重的势能，在有限步后于对角线外稳定，并在 $\mathbb{Z}_p^2$ 上一致收敛（$M$ 步后误差 $p^{-M}$）到尺度协变完美作用量，其对角线附近行为由 Volkenborn 积分决定；若带时间步权重则不存在极限。2）粗化方向：固定频率下谐振子无精化极限，但变分流闭式可解——格点角度每步乘 $p$，耦合满足 Chebyshev 递推，无焦散现象，重标度作用量收敛到自由作用量，其重整化质量为 $m_0\sin_p(\theta_0)/\theta_0$。3）线性化（重标度）映射在多项式作用量上可对角化，本征值为 $p^{2-b}$，表明在 $\mathbb{R}$ 上相关（relevant）的方向在 $\mathbb{Q}_p$ 上无关（irrelevant），反之亦然。

Conclusion: p 进设定使时间步的实范数小与 p 进范数大重合，导致精化与粗化（$\mathbb{R}$ 上的紫外与红外方向）互换角色，且各自仅给出一个极限。运动学重分块作为变分重分块的 Fréchet 导数产生了尺度协变的完美作用量；势能权重的存在与否对精化极限的存否起决定性作用；谐振子在粗化下可完全解析求解并重整化为自由作用量。线性化谱 $p^{2-b}$ 进一步揭示了实分析与 p 进分析中相关/无关方向的倒置。

Abstract: In earlier work we introduced a finite-partition propagator for $\mathbb{C}_p$-valued wave functions, in which the intermediate positions are integrated against atomic measures. The iterated integral evaluates the action on the equally spaced chain between the endpoints, so each partition level is obtained by iterating an exact, tree-level block decimation of two-point lattice actions, with no integration over fluctuations. This kinematic decimation is the Fréchet derivative, at the free actions, of the variational decimation underlying classical perfect actions. Since the time step $p^n$ is small in the real norm exactly when it is large $p$-adically, refinement and coarsening (the ultraviolet and infrared directions over $\mathbb{R}$) exchange roles, and each yields one limit. Under refinement, potentials supported on $\mathbb{Z}_p$, given there by a convergent power series, and entering the slice action without the time-step weight produce an interaction that stabilizes off the diagonal after finitely many steps and converges uniformly on $\mathbb{Z}_p^2$, with sharp error $p^{-M}$ after $M$ steps, to a scale-covariant (perfect) action, governed near the diagonal by a Volkenborn integral; with the weight no limit exists. For the harmonic oscillator no refinement limit exists at fixed frequency, but under coarsening the variational flow is solved in closed form: the lattice angle is multiplied by $p$ at each step, the coupling obeys a Chebyshev recursion, no caustic occurs, and the rescaled action converges to a free action with renormalized mass $m_0\sin_p(θ_0)/θ_0$. The rescaled linearized map is diagonalizable on polynomial actions with eigenvalues $p^{2-b}$, so directions relevant over $\mathbb{R}$ are irrelevant over $\mathbb{Q}_p$ and conversely.

</details>


### [107] [Scaling limits for products of induced symplectic Ginibre matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25885)
*Can Atacan,Noah Aygün,Yong-Woo Lee*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究多个独立诱导辛Ginibre矩阵乘积在大维极限下的局部特征值关联，建立了体部、谱边缘及谱支撑外（含实轴附近与远离实轴）关联函数的标度极限，并给出原点处参数依赖的极限Pfaffian预核的积分表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诱导辛Ginibre矩阵乘积构成的非埃尔米特随机矩阵模型，其特征值关联函数在大维极限下的普适性与局部统计行为尚未完全厘清，尤其是实轴附近的Pfaffian结构、谱边缘及原点奇异性处的极限刻画有待建立。

Method: 作者采用统一的证明框架：针对斜（skew）预核建立微分方程并研究其渐近行为，从而推导标度极限。具体涵盖体部、谱边缘、谱支撑外（实轴附近和远离实轴）及原点等区域的关联函数极限，并利用Pfaffian点过程与行列式点过程的结构刻画极限关联。

Result: 在正则谱点处，乘积模型的极限关联与单个辛Ginibre系综一致：实轴附近呈现Pfaffian结构，远离实轴处呈现行列式结构；在谱密度产生奇异性的原点处，得到参数依赖的极限Pfaffian预核的积分表示。

Conclusion: 该工作系统性地建立了诱导辛Ginibre矩阵乘积特征值局部关联的普适极限，揭示其实轴附近的Pfaffian结构与远离实轴的行列式结构，并精确刻画了原点奇异性处的预核，为乘积型非埃尔米特随机矩阵的局部普适性提供了完整图景与统一分析方法。

Abstract: We study the local eigenvalue correlations of products of independent induced symplectic Ginibre matrices in the large-dimensional limit. We establish the scaling limits of the correlation functions in the bulk, at the spectral edges, and outside the limiting spectral support, both near and away from the real axis. At regular spectral points, we show that the limiting correlations coincide with those of a single symplectic Ginibre ensemble, exhibiting Pfaffian structures near the real axis and determinantal structures away from it. At the origin, where the limiting spectral density develops a singularity, we derive an integral representation of the parameter-dependent limiting Pfaffian prekernel. Our proofs are based on a unified approach involving a differential equation for the skew prekernels and their asymptotics.

</details>


### [108] [Root-of-Unity Limits of Trigonometric qKZ Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26375)
*Alexander Varchenko*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究酉根极限下三角 qKZ 联络的行为：当乘法步长趋近于原 m 次单位根且量子群与扭转参数趋近于 1 时，m 步 qKZ 传输在固定次数子空间上给出一个微分联络，该联络是三角 KZ 联络在幂映射 z↦z^m 下的拉回，且 KZ 参数被 m 重标度。文中还建立了形式版本与 ℓ-adic 版本，并在一次情形（所有最高权为 1）给出归一化 qKZ 超几何圆周积分在任意角腔中的极限，用带载荷的超几何积分与显式的上三角矩阵表达，并证明了所得基本矩阵在向内解析延拓（受右弱 Bruhat 序支配）与幂映射（含根阶复合）下的相容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索 qKZ 方程在量子参数趋近单位根时的退化行为，将量子可积结构中的分立步骤传输与经典（三角）KZ 联络联系起来，从而理解单位根极限下量子结构与经典微分方程结构的对应关系。

Method: 在复数域上研究 U_q(𝔰𝔩_2) Verma 模张量积关联的三角 qKZ 联络及其超几何解的单位根极限。取乘法步长趋近原 m 次单位根、量子群参数与扭转参数趋近 1，考察 m 步 qKZ 传输在固定次数子空间上诱导的微分联络；证明其为三角 KZ 联络经幂映射 z↦z^m 的拉回且参数重标度为 m。利用形式变形与 ℓ-adic 技巧建立相应版本。在一次、所有最高权为 1 的情形，对归一化 qKZ 超几何圆周积分在不同角腔取极限，借助带载荷超几何积分与显式上三角矩阵描述结果，并通过右弱 Bruhat 序与幂映射（含根阶复合）验证向内解析延拓的相容性。

Result: 主要结果是：(1) m 步 qKZ 传输在单位根极限下于固定次数子空间给出微分联络，且该联络等于三角 KZ 联络在 z↦z^m 下拉回、KZ 参数乘以 m，并成立形式与 ℓ-adic 版本；(2) 在一次、最高权全为 1 时，归一化 qKZ 超几何圆周积分的极限在任意角腔中可用带载荷超几何积分与显式腔依赖上三角矩阵表示；(3) 所得特殊基本矩阵在向内解析延拓下由右弱 Bruhat 序支配，并在幂映射（含根阶复合）下相容。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了三角 qKZ 联络及其超几何解在单位根极限下的极限结构，揭示其与经典三角 KZ 方程在幂映射下的深刻对应，并通过带载荷超几何积分与上三角矩阵给出显式解，确立了基本矩阵在 Bruhat 序解析延拓与幂映射复合下的相容性，为量子与经典可积系统之间的极限联系提供了系统框架。

Abstract: Over the field of complex numbers, we study root-of-unity limits of trigonometric qKZ connections associated with tensor products of $U_q(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ Verma modules and of their hypergeometric solutions. As the multiplicative step approaches a primitive $m$-th root of unity and the quantum-group and twist parameters approach $1$, the $m$-step qKZ transport yields, on every fixed-degree subspace,a differential connection. We show that this connection is the pullback of the trigonometric KZ connection under the power map $z\mapsto z^m$, with the KZ parameter rescaled by $m$, and establish formal and $\ell$-adic versions.
  In degree one, with all highest weights equal to $1$, we determine the limits of normalized qKZ hypergeometric circle integrals in arbitrary angular chambers. The limits are expressed in terms of loaded hypergeometric integrals for the trigonometric KZ equations and explicit chamber-dependent upper-triangular matrices. We establish compatibility of the resulting distinguished fundamental matrices under inward analytic continuation, governed by the right weak Bruhat order,and under power maps, including composition of root orders.

</details>


### [109] [Curvature Integrability in Twinor Theory: An Ambitwistor Approach to the Twinor Googly Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26399)
*Chen-Hsu Chien*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于双扭量(twinor)与ambitwistor结构的框架，用零自平行线(null autoparallels)处理googly问题，在特定商、求值与下降假设下重建双扭量场方程及能量-动量方程等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决googly问题的twinor类比，即在不依赖手性零曲面、同时保留两种手性、源与挠率的条件下，为双扭量结构提供一个ambitwistor构造方案。

Method: 利用由零自平行线定义的ambitwistor结构；在商、求值与下降假设下，通过全纯仿射截面重建场方程；借助对应空间上同调实现压缩手性方程与阶梯方程；对给定的非正规共形联络，用无穷扭量平行性恢复能量-动量方程并补充独立自旋流方程。

Result: 得到条件性的辅助表述：全纯仿射截面重建双扭量场方程DY=J；对应空间上同调实现含焊接耦合与质量归一化源的压缩手性方程和阶梯方程；无穷扭量平行性恢复能量-动量方程与独立自旋流方程。

Conclusion: 该构造提供了一个有条件的辅助公式化方案，但本构源与引力规定仍需作为动力学输入，尚未在框架内确定。

Abstract: The twinor analogue of the googly problem is addressed through an ambitwistor structure defined by null autoparallels, retaining both chiralities, sources and torsion without requiring chiral null surfaces. Under the stated quotient, evaluation and descent assumptions, holomorphic affine sections reconstruct the twinor field equation $DY=J$. Correspondence-space cohomology realizes the contracted chiral and ladder equations, including the ladder's soldering coupling and mass-normalization source. For a prescribed non-normal conformal connection, infinity-twistor parallelism recovers the energy--momentum equation, supplemented by independent spin-current equations. These constructions give a conditional auxiliary formulation; constitutive sources and gravitational prescriptions remain dynamical input.

</details>
